Modern pretrained encoders often learn features that exhibit disentangled properties without making generative assumptions, yet there is no general theory for interpreting these features as independent factors of variation. We take a step toward such a theory by introducing Riemannian ICA (RICA), which replaces ICA's global generative model with local geometric structure. RICA is founded on the observation that in ICA, the factors of variation underlying a data point can be understood through radial curves emanating from the point that map to axis-aligned lines in the latent space. We formalize this perspective using Riemannian geometry and introduce our theory in a way that is consistent with the existing generative approach. Our main contribution is the disentanglement tensor, which encodes a second-order notion of disentanglement that we call pointwise disentanglement. This tensor depends on the Hessian of the data log likelihood as well as the Ricci curvature induced by the model. In a controlled source recovery setting with known ground-truth sources, RICA recovers sources across several manifolds, while the success of ICA baselines depends on the coordinates used to represent the observations. Our work provides a theoretical basis for studying local disentanglement without assuming a global generative model.

</details>


### [107] [Relational Linear Properties in Language Models: An Empirical Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22532)
*Giovanni Valer,Luigi Gresele,Marco Bronzini,Emanuele Marconato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KL散度的探针方法，检验语言模型表示中的关系线性性假设（即固定关系可通过线性映射从主语嵌入预测宾语去嵌入），发现该性质在不同模型、层和关系表述方式上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的表示中存在线性性质，但如何实验性地测试这些性质仍具挑战性。本文旨在验证Marconato等人（2025）提出的关系线性性假设，并克服先前方法（如Jacobian近似）的不足。

Method: 提出基于KL散度的探针方法，用于评估关系线性性。该方法避免了Hernandez等人（2024）线性关系嵌入中的粗糙Jacobian近似，效率更高。实验在四个数据集上，跨不同层和不同释义的关系查询进行测试。

Result: 关系线性性在不同模型中表现各异；存在与先前关于模型表示中语言信息的观察一致的逐层模式；关系表述方式的变化对线性性的影响程度不同。

Conclusion: 关系线性性并非所有模型或所有层中都稳定成立，其受到模型架构、层深度和查询表述方式的显著影响，提示未来研究需考虑这些因素。

Abstract: Linear properties are ubiquitous in the representations of language models; however, testing them experimentally remains a challenging task. This work focuses on relational linearity: the hypothesis that, for a fixed relation (e.g., "plays"), the unembedding of an object (e.g., "trumpet") can be predicted from the embedding of its subject (e.g.,"Miles Davis") by a linear map. We present an experimental method to test the formulation of relational linearity by Marconato et al. (2025). Specifically, we introduce a probing method, based on Kullback-Leibler divergence, to evaluate this property and examine its variation across layers and paraphrased relational queries. It is also more efficient than previous work; for example, it avoids the crude Jacobian approximations used in Linear Relational Embeddings by Hernandez et al. (2024). Our findings across four datasets show that relational linearity varies across models, exhibits layer-wise patterns consistent with prior observations about linguistic information in model representations, and is differently affected by changes in how the relation is phrased.

</details>


### [108] [F-TIS: Harnessing Diverse Models in Collaborative GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22537)
*Nikolay Blagoev,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Wendelin Boehmer,Lydia Yiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出F-TIS方法，使异构模型能在GRPO训练中高效协作，利用离策略样本实现与在策略训练相同的收敛效果，并在某些分布外任务上提升高达12%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO训练依赖同构模型分布式推理以保持样本在策略性，这在去中心化系统中不切实际，因为不同计算资源和偏好的节点需要协作训练，而异构模型会产生离策略样本损害GRPO收敛。

Method: 提出Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS)，一种GRPO风格训练范式，通过过滤和截断重要性采样，使异构模型产生的离策略样本能被有效用于本地模型学习，同时保持通信高效。

Result: F-TIS在多种异构设置下实现与纯在策略训练相同的最终模型收敛；在某些分布外任务上性能提升高达12%，表现出更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: F-TIS有效解决了异构模型协作GRPO训练的离策略样本问题，不仅收敛不变，还能提升泛化性能，为去中心化联邦RL训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO have seen great popularity in LLM post-training. In GRPO, models produce completions to a set of prompts, which are rewarded, and the policy is updated towards the relatively high reward completions. Due to the auto-regressive nature of models, the generation phase of such style of training can be extremely time consuming. As a solution, prior work has sought to distribute the inference step across many nodes, working parallel. These works assume primarily homogeneous models in the training in order to keep samples as close to on-policy as possible. This assumption may be impractical in decentralized systems, where parties with various computes and preferences may wish to collaborate on the same task. Thus, decentralized training requires an approach that can handle heterogeneous models - different models collaborating on the same tasks. However, this leads to highly off-policy samples presented during training, which prior work has identified that off-policy samples can hurt GRPO convergence. To enable heterogeneity, we propose Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS) - a GRPO-style training paradigm that can use off-policy samples to improve local model's learning. Our framework allows various models to collaborate in the same RL training run while being communication efficient. We extensively evaluate F-TIS in various heterogeneous setups and we show that it exhibits identical final model convergence to purely on-sample training. Furthermore, we observe in some setups better generalization on out-of-distribution tasks than on-policy training, increasing model's performance by up to 12\%.

</details>


### [109] [ImplicitTerrainV2: Wavelet-Guided Spatially Adaptive Neural Terrain Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22556)
*Haoan Feng,Xin Xu,Leila De Floriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ImplicitTerrainV2，一种结合频谱控制、小波引导空间自适应、导数感知监督和模型压缩的紧凑高效神经地形数据格式，在50个瑞士地形块上达到66.25 dB PSNR，比先前工作提高5.70 dB，参数减少3.2倍，训练时间每块55秒，存储压缩至1.23 bpp，支持离群点查询、闭式导数计算和分辨率无关重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有栅格DEM依赖插值和有限差分算子，而先前的神经隐式表示（INR）地形方法缺乏显式频率控制、忽略地形梯度结构，且模型过大、训练成本高，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出小波复杂度场（WCF）从解析小波系数导出空间自适应频率掩码，将高频容量定位到复杂地形区域；基于WCF的复杂度感知自适应采样集中训练于高复杂度区域；梯度匹配提供额外监督以保持地形平滑流形结构；训练后混合精度量化和熵编码实现存储压缩。

Result: 在50个瑞士地形块上达到66.25 dB端到端PSNR，比先前工作提高5.70 dB，参数减少3.2倍，单GPU训练每块55秒；压缩至1.23 bpp仅损失0.28 dB PSNR；率失真性能与多种已建立DEM编解码器相当。

Conclusion: ImplicitTerrainV2提供了一种紧凑、高效的神经地形数据格式，兼具高保真度、低存储、快速训练的优点，并支持离群点查询、闭式导数计算和分辨率无关重建，有望惠及多种下游GIS应用。

Abstract: Digital elevation models (DEMs) underpin terrain analysis in Geographic Information Systems (GIS), but in their common raster form, they rely on interpolation for off-grid sampling and finite-difference operators for derivative-based analysis. Implicit neural representations (INRs) offer a continuous alternative, but prior terrain INRs lack explicit frequency control, neglect the gradient structure of terrain, and remain too large and costly to train for practical deployment. We present ImplicitTerrainV2, which advances terrain INRs toward a compact, efficient neural terrain data format by combining a spectral control mechanism with wavelet-guided spatial adaptivity, derivative-aware supervision, and post-training model compression. At its core, a wavelet complexity field (WCF) derives spatially-adaptive frequency masks from analytically computed wavelet coefficients, localizing high-frequency capacity to complex terrain regions. The same field guides complexity-aware adaptive sampling that concentrates training in high-complexity regions, while gradient matching applies extra supervision to enforce the smooth manifold structure of terrain DEMs for improved derivative fidelity. Post-training mixed-precision quantization and entropy coding reduce storage to 1.23 bpp with a 0.28 dB PSNR drop. On 50 Swiss terrain tiles, ImplicitTerrainV2 reaches 66.25 dB end-to-end PSNR, improving over the prior work by 5.70 dB while using 3.2x fewer parameters and training in 55 s per tile on a single GPU. Our compressed neural format is competitive with several established DEM codecs in rate-distortion performance, while additionally supporting off-grid point queries, closed-form derivative evaluation, and resolution-independent reconstruction, which may benefit many downstream GIS applications.

</details>


### [110] [Regret-Based $(ε,δ)$-optimal Stopping Criteria for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22561)
*Haowei Wang,Jingyi Wang,Qiyu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对贝叶斯优化中缺乏理论支撑的停止准则问题，提出了基于GP-UCB的瞬时遗憾更紧界，并据此设计了能在高概率下保证ε-最优解的停止准则，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化通常固定预算后终止，既可能浪费资源又无法保证解的质量。现有停止准则缺乏理论保证，因此需要一种理论严谨的停止准则来在保证解质量的同时避免不必要的计算开销。

Method: 首先推导了GP-UCB在任意迭代时刻更紧的瞬时遗憾上界，然后基于此上界设计了停止准则，使得算法终止时能以至少1-δ的概率保证得到ε-最优解。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提停止准则的有效性和高效性，能够在保证解质量的前提下提前终止不必要的迭代。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于更紧瞬时遗憾界的停止准则为GP-UCB提供了理论上有保障的终止条件，在实用性和理论性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a widely used iterative black-box optimization method that utilizes Gaussian process (GP) surrogate models. In practice, BO is typically terminated after a fixed evaluation budget is exhausted, which can incur unnecessary cost and provides no optimality guarantee on solution quality. Recent research in developing a practical stopping criterion has made empirical progress, yet a theoretically sound stopping criterion remains a work in progress. In this work, we present provably tighter instantaneous regret bounds for GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) at any given iteration. Then, we propose stopping criteria for GP-UCB based on this tighter bound that ensures an $ε$-optimal solution with high probability $1-δ$ upon termination. Numerical experiments are performed to validate and demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our stopping criteria.

</details>


### [111] [GraphFlow: A Graph-Based Workflow Management for Efficient LLM-Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22566)
*Ao Li,Shangpeng Yang,Fahao Chen,Tianheng Xu,Peng Li,Zhou Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于统一图（wGraph）的工作流管理范式GraphFlow，用于动态生成任务特定工作流并高效管理KV缓存，在五个基准数据集上平均性能提升约4.95个百分点，内存占用减少约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体系统依赖预定义模板和浅层匹配机制，难以捕获深层语义关系，无法泛化到未见过的任务。

Method: 提出wGraph统一图表示工作流，每个节点对应原子操作；基于此设计GraphFlow系统，包含自适应工作流生成（根据任务语义和约束动态构建工作流）和工作流状态管理（利用wGraph结构高效管理KV缓存，减少冗余计算）。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，平均性能提升约4.95个百分点，内存占用减少约4倍。

Conclusion: GraphFlow通过动态工作流生成和高效状态管理，显著优于现有方法，在性能和资源效率上均有大幅提升。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents demonstrate strong reasoning and execution capabilities on complex tasks when guided by structured instructions, commonly referred to as workflows. However, existing workflow-assisted agent serving systems typically rely on predefined templates and shallow matching mechanisms, which limit their ability to capture deep semantic relationships and generalize to previously unseen tasks. To address these limitations, we propose a new workflow management paradigm that represents workflows using a unified graph, termed wGraph, where each node corresponds to an atomic operation. wGraph serves as a shared substrate from which task-specific workflows are dynamically instantiated. Building on wGraph primitives, we introduce GraphFlow, a system that efficiently integrates workflows into agent serving through two key designs. First, adaptive workflow generation dynamically constructs workflows from wGraph based on task semantics and constraint requirements. Second, workflow state management exploits wGraph structure to efficiently manage Key-Value (KV) caches, reducing redundant computation during agent serving. Extensive experiments across five benchmark datasets show that GraphFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding an average performance improvement of approximately 4.95 percentage points, while achieving an approximately 4$\times$ reduction in memory footprint.

</details>


### [112] [A Tutorial on Diffusion Theory: From Differential Equations to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22586)
*Jiayi Fu,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本教程从微分方程视角系统阐述了扩散模型，涵盖前向/反向ODE与SDE公式、分数匹配训练目标、各类采样方法（DPM-Solver、分类器引导等），并揭示了DDPM与DDIM在统一框架下的本质联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从微分方程的统一数学视角重新梳理扩散模型的理论基础，打通前向过程、反向过程、训练目标与采样方法之间的逻辑脉络，帮助读者建立对扩散模型更系统、更深刻的理解。

Method: 1) 从条件高斯前向过程出发，推导其ODE和SDE两种表示；2) 通过对数据分布求平均得到边缘化的前向ODE/SDE；3) 推导反向SDE和概率流ODE，揭示其由边缘分数$\grad\log p_t(x)$控制；4) 建立分数匹配与噪声预测目标的等价性；5) 介绍DPM-Solver、分类器引导和无分类器引导等采样方法；6) 将DDPM/DDIM分别对应到反向SDE/ODE的离散化采样。

Result: 系统建立了扩散模型的微分方程理论框架，证明了噪声预测目标与分数匹配等价（相差一个与模型参数无关的加性常数），并阐明DDPM采样等价于离散反向SDE采样，DDIM采样等价于反向ODE采样。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可统一在微分方程框架下理解，前向与反向过程有清晰的ODE/SDE对应关系；采样方法可区分为基于反向SDE（如DDPM）和基于反向概率流ODE（如DDIM）两大类，两类方法共享同一训练目标。

Abstract: This tutorial develops diffusion models from the viewpoint of differential equations. We begin with the conditional Gaussian forward process and show that this path admits both an ordinary differential equation (ODE) representation and a stochastic differential equation (SDE) representation. Averaging the conditional process over the data distribution then yields marginalized forward ODE and SDE formulations that transport the data distribution $p_0=p_{\mathrm{data}}$ to a Gaussian prior $p_1=\mathcal{N}(0,I)$. We next derive the corresponding reverse-time dynamics, namely the reverse SDE and the reverse probability-flow ODE, both of which are governed by the marginal score $\grad\log p_t(x)$. This leads to a training objective for score estimation and shows that the standard noise-prediction objective is equivalent to score matching up to an additive constant independent of the model parameters. We then discuss sampling methods for the learned reverse dynamics, including DPM-Solver, as well as guided sampling through classifier guidance and classifier-free guidance. Finally, we compare DDPM and DDIM with the reverse SDE/ODE framework and show that they share the same training objective, while DDPM sampling corresponds to discrete reverse-SDE sampling and DDIM sampling corresponds to reverse-ODE sampling.

</details>


### [113] [Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization for LLM-Guided Program Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22613)
*Halil Alperen Gozeten,Xuechen Zhang,Emrullah Ildiz,Ege Onur Taga,Tara Javidi,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EMO-STA框架，通过两阶段（共享演化+任务适应）实现LLM引导的跨任务程序发现，在8个任务族上超越单任务演化，并证明共享演化能缓解过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的演化搜索方法对每个任务独立优化，未能利用相关任务间可复用的结构信息，导致计算效率低下且易过拟合。

Method: 提出EMO-STA两阶段框架：阶段一在任务族上共享演化生成可执行程序档案；阶段二通过多种适应策略（热启动、最佳平均程序适应、目标最优程序适应）将共享候选适应到具体任务。

Result: 在连续优化、几何构造、建模、算法优化等8个任务族上，EMO-STA在多数设置下优于同等计算量的单任务演化；STA Best-Local在分布内适应最强，STA Best-Shared在未见任务上泛化鲁棒；预算分配实验表明共享演化应占较大比重。

Conclusion: 跨任务共享演化不仅能提升计算效率，还能通过在低证据场景（如少样本ARC任务、时序特征工程）中偏好跨任务泛化程序来缓解过拟合。

Abstract: Recent LLM-guided evolutionary search methods have shown that iterative program mutation can discover strong algorithms, but they typically optimize each task independently, even when related tasks share reusable structure. We introduce Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization (EMO) for LLM-guided program discovery, and propose EMO-STA (Shared-Then-Adapt), a two-stage framework that first evolves a shared archive of executable programs across a task family and then adapts selected shared candidates to each target task. Within EMO-STA, we explore multiple adaptation strategies, including warm-starting from the shared archive, adapting the best average shared program, and adapting the shared program that performs best on each target task. Across eight task families spanning continuous optimization, geometric construction, modeling, and algorithmic optimization, EMO-STA improves over matched-compute single-task evolution in most settings, with STA Best-Local providing the strongest in-distribution adaptation and STA Best-Shared yielding robust transfer to unseen tasks. Compute-allocation experiments show that allocating a substantial fraction of the family-level budget to shared evolution is consistently beneficial, with roughly balanced shared and adaptation budgets often being optimal. Beyond compute efficiency, we show that shared evolution can mitigate overfitting in low-evidence settings (e.g. few training data), including ARC tasks and time-series feature engineering, by favoring programs that generalize across all tasks rather than exploiting task-specific brittle artifacts.

</details>


### [114] [Two is better than one: A Collapse-free Multi-Reward RLIF Training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22620)
*Shourov Joarder,Diganta Sikdar,Ahsan Habib Akash,Binod Bhattarai,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多奖励RLIF框架，通过聚类投票的答案级奖励和基于token自置信度的完成级奖励，结合GDPO归一化和KL-Cov正则化，在无外部监督下实现接近监督RLVR的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLIF方法依赖单一内部奖励，容易导致奖励破解、熵崩溃和推理结构退化，亟需更稳定的无监督替代方案。

Method: 1) 双通道奖励：答案级（聚类投票）和完成级（token自置信度）；2) GDPO归一化处理奖励尺度不平衡；3) KL-Cov正则化针对低熵token分布，防止熵崩溃。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，稳定性和鲁棒性优于现有无监督RL方法，性能接近有监督RLVR方法。

Conclusion: 互补的内部奖励结合针对性正则化，可以在无需外部真实标注的情况下支持稳定的长程推理。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of LLMs, but often depends on external supervision from human annotations or gold-standard solutions. Reinforcement learning from internal feedback (RLIF) has recently emerged as a scalable unsupervised alternative, using signals extracted from the model itself. However, existing RLIF methods typically rely on a single internal reward, which can lead to reward hacking, entropy collapse, and degraded reasoning structure. We propose a multi-reward RLIF framework that decomposes the training signal into two complementary components: an answer-level reward based on cluster voting and a completion-level reward based on token-wise self-certainty. To combine these signals robustly, we apply GDPO-based normalization to reduce reward-scale imbalance. We further introduce KL-Cov regularization, which targets low-entropy token distributions responsible for disproportionate entropy reduction, preserving exploration and preventing late-stage collapse. Across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, our method improves stability and robustness over prior unsupervised RL approaches, while achieving performance close to supervised RLVR methods. These results show that complementary internal rewards, combined with targeted regularization, can support stable long-horizon reasoning without relying on external ground-truth supervision. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [115] [A note on convergence of Wasserstein policy optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22622)
*David Šiška,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程框架下，Wasserstein策略优化（WPO）算法具有线性收敛性，通过利用均值场分析和对数Sobolev不等式建立理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管WPO在连续动作空间的随机策略优化中取得了实证成功，但其在连续状态和动作空间环境下的理论收敛性尚未得到充分建立，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 利用均值场分析的最新进展，结合对数Sobolev不等式分析梯度流的收敛性，假设梯度流方程存在足够正则的解，证明沿流单调能量耗散并建立局部对数Sobolev不等式。

Result: 证明了在熵正则化MDP框架下，WPO的价值函数能够线性收敛到全局最优解。

Conclusion: WPO算法在理论层面具有线性收敛保证，为其在连续控制任务中的应用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Wasserstein Policy Optimization (WPO) is a recently proposed reinforcement learning algorithm that leverages Wasserstein gradient flows to optimize stochastic policies in continuous action spaces. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical convergence properties of WPO in environments with continuous state and action spaces have yet to be fully established. In this note, we argue that WPO within the framework of entropy-regularised Markov Decision Processes converges linearly. This is done by leveraging recent advances in mean-field analysis for convergence of gradient flows using log-Sobole inequalities. Assuming existence of sufficiently regular solution to the gradient flow equation we demonstrate monotonic energy dissipation along the flow and establish a local log-Sobolev inequality. Ultimately, these properties allow us to argue that the value function should converge linearly to the global optimum.

</details>


### [116] [Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22691)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了β-VAE中的后验坍缩实现了自动谱剪枝，揭示了潜在模式按有用性依次解耦的级联坍缩现象，并建立了与PCA谱的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解β-VAE中后验坍缩的本质机制，解释为什么增大β会导致潜在变量依次失效，以及如何利用这一现象进行自动化的表示学习与特征选择。

Method: 通过Landau稳定性分析推导损失函数的平衡态解，定义潜在重缩放不变序参量对活跃潜在模式排序，并在线性高斯情形下与PCA谱进行理论对比。

Result: 后验坍缩实现了自动谱剪枝；坍缩阈值与β相关，潜在模式从最不有用到最有用依次解耦；线性高斯情况下坍缩谱、效用谱与归一化PCA谱一致，坍缩服从平均场规律；WorldClim数据集验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: β-VAE的后验坍缩并非缺陷，而是一种有益的正则化机制，可自动识别哪些潜在变量值得保留，为可解释表示学习提供理论依据。

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [117] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出GRPO风格目标中的硬裁剪（hard clipping）是RLVR训练不稳定的关键瓶颈，导致边界附近的有效信号被丢弃。提出近边界随机救援（NSR）方法，通过随机保留略超出裁剪边界的token来恢复丢失信号。NSR可解释为在期望意义上引入隐式梯度衰减，但其随机的边界局部救援机制优于确定性梯度衰减。在7B-30B模型、密集和MoE架构上，NSR显著提升训练稳定性并在DAPO、GSPO等强基线上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽已成为扩展LLM推理能力的核心范式，但其优化过程存在训练不稳定和次优收敛的问题。作者通过对裁剪式GRPO目标进行系统剖析，识别出硬裁剪导致的刚性裁剪决策是这一问题的关键实际瓶颈。

Method: 提出近边界随机救援（NSR），一种即插即用的最小化修改：对略超出裁剪阈值的token进行随机保留（随机采样），从而恢复丢失的信号。该方法可被解释为在期望中引入隐式梯度衰减，但核心在于其随机的、边界局部的救援机制。

Result: 在7B到30B模型规模、密集和MoE架构上，NSR作为即插即用方案显著提升了训练稳定性，并在DAPO、GSPO等强基线上取得一致的性能增益。消融实验表明，其随机的边界局部救援机制始终优于确定性的梯度衰减。

Conclusion: 硬裁剪的刚性决策是RLVR训练的关键瓶颈，边界附近的信号对优化有重要价值。NSR通过简单的随机扰动即可有效恢复这些信号，为RLVR提供了一种稳定且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [118] [Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22711)
*Clarisse Wibault,Alexander Goldie,Antonio Villares,Maike Osborne,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在离线目标条件强化学习中，层级策略不仅能减少时间跨度，还能通过相对化选项和不同层级的不同表征实现绝对抽象，从而跨相似上下文重用经验，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDP在真实世界的GCRL中常因对称性和跨状态-目标对的共享结构而存在显著冗余，现有工作虽将层级策略用于时间抽象，但未充分挖掘其在绝对抽象方面的潜力。

Method: 引入相对化选项（relativised options）以及为层级中不同层次使用不同表征，基于此框架提出两种简单算法，分别用于学习相对化选项和从绝对参考系中进行抽象。

Result: 实验表明，引入的归纳偏置在离线GCRL中显著提升了性能。

Conclusion: 层级策略不仅能实现时间抽象（缩减时间跨度），还能实现绝对抽象，通过相对化选项和分层表征可有效跨相似上下文重用经验，提升离线GCRL性能。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.

</details>


### [119] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项关于GPT-2 Small在间接宾语识别任务中，稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征在不同成败试验中的激活差异审计。发现24,576个特征中有105个达到大效应量。最显著的相关特征是17,491（“加密密钥”），但因果消融未恢复性能，表明其仅为相关性而非因果关系。贡献在于提出了一种廉价、模型无关的审计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型在特定任务（IOI）上的失败模式，探究SAE特征是否可解释模型行为差异，并验证这些特征是因果关系还是仅相关性。

Method: 使用Bloom(2024)发布的GPT-2 Small第8层残差流的SAE特征，对300个IOI提示进行审计。统计筛选Holm校正后显著的特征，计算Cohen's d效应量。对最强相关特征进行三项控制检验：（i）因果消融；（ii）表示基线比较（逻辑回归 vs SAE特征）；（iii）种子鲁棒性检验。

Result: 300个提示中准确率79.7%；146个特征通过Holm校正，105个达到大效应量（|d|>0.8）。特征17,491（d=+2.93）在转移对象为"the keys"时几乎唯一激活，该条件下失败率93.3% vs 其他对象7.5%。消融后准确率从6.7%降至4.4%（未恢复）；逻辑回归AUC 0.929与SAE前100特征0.927持平；种子鲁棒性检验中该特征仅在1/5次运行中为top特征。

Conclusion: 该方法的贡献在于提出了一套廉价、模型无关、可复现的审计流程（可在笔记本上运行），而非发现某个具体特征。强相关特征17,491是行为相关物而非因果因素，SAE基座增加了可解释性但未增加预测能力。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [120] [The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22723)
*Md Sahil Akhtar,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias,Adam D. Jozefiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 高斯DDPM中，匹配完整后验协方差可将路径KL散度从不可避免的Ω(1/T)降至O(1/T²)；为此提出Lanczos高斯采样器(LGS)，一种免训练、免矩阵的采样方法，仅用少量雅可比-向量乘积即可实现指数级逼近，经验上三步即超越强对角协方差基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准各向同性逆协方差在路径空间KL散度上存在不可避免的Ω(1/T)误差，这对于分类器引导等涉及整个反向轨迹的流程尤为关键；需要找到一种方法突破这一瓶颈，实现更优的路径KL收敛率。

Method: 提出Lanczos高斯采样器(LGS)，利用Lanczos方法通过协方差-向量乘积（通过后验均值的雅可比-向量乘积获得）来采样最优逆协方差，无需存储稠密协方差矩阵或训练辅助协方差模型。

Result: 理论证明匹配完整后验协方差将路径KL降至O(1/T²)，LGS近似误差随Lanczos步数指数衰减；仅用3步Lanczos即可在标准图像基准上超越OCM-DDPM等强对角协方差基线。

Conclusion: 全协方差匹配在理论上具有价值，在实践中对于快速DDPM采样也是可行的，为提升扩散模型采样质量提供了新途径。

Abstract: A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.

</details>


### [121] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多任务算子学习（Multi-Task Operator Learning）的逼近与统计复杂度，证明多任务神经算子（MNO）架构在共享表示下不会增加整体学习成本，其缩放定律与单任务算子学习一致，并与多任务DeepONet在渐近逼近率上等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多任务设置下算子学习的逼近复杂度与统计泛化能力，探究共享表示是否会增加整体的学习代价。

Method: 针对Lipschitz多算子映射类，推导MNO架构在逼近与统计泛化上的近最优上界；同时建立参数复杂度的诅咒并证明极小化最优下界，将MNO与基于拼接任务输入的多任务DeepONet进行最坏情况下的逼近复杂度比较。

Result: 多任务算子学习与单任务算子学习遵循相同的缩放定律，共享表示不会增加整体成本；MNO与多任务DeepONet在最坏情况逼近复杂度上具有相同的渐近率。

Conclusion: 共享多任务表示在算子学习中不会带来额外的参数或统计代价，两种主流多任务算子架构在逼近复杂度上渐近等价。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [122] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三元决策树，通过在分裂节点中引入以最优阈值为中心、半宽为delta的不确定性区域，对落入该区域的实例进行加权混合预测并标记为边界不确定。基于CART分裂时已有的统计量，提出了五种delta估计方法。在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上的5折交叉验证中，所有方法在决定准确率上均显著优于标准CART（p<0.001）。其中margin方法效率最佳（每单位边界不确定标记率带来0.104准确率提升），在42/72数据集上胜出且无需额外超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树使用硬二值阈值划分特征空间，对远离决策边界和恰在决策边界上的实例赋予相同的置信度，这忽略了边界区域的不确定性，可能导致不恰当的预测和下游应用决策。

Method: 提出三元决策树框架：每个分裂节点增加一个以最优阈值为中心、半宽为delta的不确定性区域，落在此区域的实例通过加权混合左右子树预测得到结果，并被标记为边界不确定。delta基于CART分裂时的已有统计量局部计算，无需额外噪声参数。提出五种delta估计方法：quality-plateau（分裂准则曲线平台宽度）、class-overlap（类分布经验重叠度）、gain-ratio（分裂质量相对分裂熵）、node-bootstrap（节点级重采样下的阈值方差）、margin（SVM启发的到异类训练样本最近距离）。采用概率路由机制进行预测混合。

Result: 在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上，五种方法均显著优于标准CART。margin方法效率最高（每单位标记率增益0.104准确率）、在42/72数据集上胜出且零额外超参数。在Breiman合成基准上，margin在干净数据上自我校准，node-bootstrap和quality-plateau最佳跟踪理论不可约误差。在医疗和金融数据集上，如乳腺X光筛查中node-bootstrap以标记10.8%样本为代价提升0.71%决定准确率。

Conclusion: 三元决策树通过局部计算的delta不确定性区域和概率路由，有效识别边界不确定实例并提升整体精度。margin方法在效率和普适性上最优，而不同delta估计方法在不同场景下各具优势，为实践者提供了灵活的选择。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [123] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeqLoRA，一种基于约束持续学习框架的参数高效微调方法，通过双层优化联合优化LoRA因子，实现多概念文本到图像扩散模型的高效组合，有效缓解表示干扰和灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽能快速个性化文本到图像扩散模型，但组合多个自定义概念时面临表示干扰问题。现有模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后验融合，要么冻结适应子空间，限制了表达能力和概念保真度。为此，需要一种同时兼顾表达效率与概念保真度的新方法。

Method: 提出SeqLoRA（序贯正则化LoRA），一种约束持续学习框架，通过双层优化联合优化LoRA的两个因子。理论层面，证明了算法的强收敛保证，并将残差层激活建模为矩阵子高斯过程，推导了灾难性遗忘的高概率界。进一步证明，从数据中学习LoRA基比冻结基方法能更有效地最小化残差干扰能量。

Result: 在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA在多达101个概念的组合场景下提升了身份保持能力和可扩展性，避免了昂贵的融合操作，并减少了组合生成中的属性干扰。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA通过持续学习框架有效解决了多概念组合中的表示干扰和灾难性遗忘问题，在保持概念保真度的同时实现了良好的可扩展性，为参数高效微调中的概念组合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [124] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对证据深度学习（EDL）中Dirichlet期望目标函数计算复杂的问题，提出用Dirichlet均值处的插件损失来近似原始目标，证明了在温和假设下近似误差随证据增长而衰减，并在语音指令数据集上验证了其与经典EDL相当的预测性能和选择性预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDL通过Dirichlet分布提供单次不确定性估计，但其期望目标函数比标准监督学习损失更复杂，导致分析和实现困难。作者旨在简化EDL的目标函数，使其更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程实现。

Method: 通过将EDL诱导的一阶经验风险最小化问题的目标近似为Dirichlet均值处的插件损失（plug-in loss），并在宽泛的损失函数类（包括均方误差和交叉熵损失）下证明近似误差随证据增长而衰减。该框架在特定证据到Dirichlet映射下包含了标准softmax分类器。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上，简化目标函数在预测精度和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，同时更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程实现。这是首个通过EDL在语音识别任务上获得覆盖-准确率权衡曲线的实证分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的简化目标函数为EDL提供了一种更易于实现且理论上合理的替代方案，在保持不确定性估计能力的同时大幅降低了计算和实现复杂度。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [125] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lumberjack，一种差分隐私随机森林算法，通过构建大型随机决策树并执行隐私保护剪枝，结合新颖的层次数据重击者检测算法，实现了当前最优的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私随机森林方法通常严重降低模型性能至不可用程度，需要一种在保护隐私的同时保持高实用性的方法。

Method: 构建大型随机决策树，然后进行激进的隐私保护剪枝以仅保留充分填充的节点；核心是提出一种适用于层次数据的$(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP重击者检测算法，其误差为$O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$（h为树高）。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，Lumberjack一致优于先前的DP随机森林方法，建立了新的最先进水平；在实用隐私预算下显著改善了隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 精心设计的DP随机森林可以弥合大部分效用差距，指出了未来研究中一个有前景且未充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [126] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了均匀扩散模型（UDM）中标准插件桥参数化与去噪后验之间的不匹配问题，提出了留一法后验参数化、基于留一法预测的推断改进（预测-校正采样器、温度采样），以及均匀扩散的吸收态重构方法，在语言建模任务上证明了留一法参数化持续提升UDM性能，吸收态重构可匹配或超越掩码扩散模型，表明掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间的经验差距主要源于参数化和采样设计而非边际分布本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型通常通过干净数据预测来训练，但预测结果在定义反向动力学时存在多种使用方式。掩码扩散模型（MDM）中这些选择基本一致，而均匀扩散模型（UDM）中则存在分歧。标准UDM的插件桥参数化并非由去噪后验优化，而是由留一法后验优化（该后验在预测每个干净token时不使用自身的噪声观测），这揭示了插件ELBO与常规交叉熵去噪目标之间的不匹配问题。

Method: (1) 理论分析了UDM中插件桥参数化与去噪后验之间的不匹配，刻画了留一法后验目标并推导了去噪器、留一法后验与得分之间的精确转换；(2) 提出了基于留一法预测的预测-校正采样器和改进的温度采样方法，无需额外训练即可提升推理效果；(3) 提出了均匀扩散的吸收态重构方法，在保持UDM联合分布的同时将其分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作，具有更简单的去噪后验、延续去掩码和自然重掩码机制。

Result: 在语言建模任务上，留一法参数化持续提升了UDM的生成效果；吸收态构造方法能够匹配或超越掩码扩散模型的性能。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间的经验差距主要源于参数化和采样设计，而非边际分布本身的选择。留一法参数化和吸收态重构为弥合这一差距提供了有效的理论和方法。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [127] [SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22776)
*Stanislav R. Kirpichenko,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Lev V. Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生存扩散概率模型（SDPM），一种基于去噪扩散模型的连续时间生存分析方法，无需参数假设或时间离散化，在十组真实数据集上取得竞争性预测性能，并能更准确地恢复连续生存分布的形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析方法要么对风险函数施加结构假设，要么将时间轴离散化，这限制了模型的灵活性并引入了近似误差。为此，作者希望提出一种无需参数假设和离散化的生成式连续时间生存分析方法。

Method: SDPM使用去噪扩散模型建模生存结果（观测时间和删失指示符）的条件分布；在条件独立删失假设下，利用Kaplan-Meier估计器将生成的条件样本转换为生存函数估计；模型在标准化对数时间构成的变换目标空间中操作，并使用连续高斯混合表示删失指示符。

Result: 在十组真实数据集上与五种强基线（树模型、梯度提升、神经生存模型）对比，SDPM在C-index、时变AUC和综合Brier分数上取得竞争性表现；在合成Cox-Weibull数据上，SDPM能比强非参数基线更准确地恢复连续生存分布形状；消融实验证实目标空间变换能改善事件率校准、减少无效生成时间并提升预测区分度。

Conclusion: SDPM作为一种无需参数假设和离散化的生成式连续时间生存分析方法，在预测性能和分布恢复能力上均具优势，且目标空间变换对模型性能至关重要。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.

</details>


### [128] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“匹配原则”：许多看似独立的鲁棒性方法（如对抗训练、域自适应、IRM等）本质上是同一个统计问题——估计标签保持部署扰动的协方差，然后沿其范围正则化编码器Jacobian矩阵。在线性高斯模型中证明了闭式最优性，并引入无标签嵌入敏感性探针TDI。13项预注册实验（含Qwen2.5-7B）验证了理论预测，12项通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒性、域自适应、光度与遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全性和经典各向异性正则化通常被视为独立问题，分别有各自的方法族。本文旨在揭示它们共享的统计结构，将这些分散的研究方向统一在一个理论框架下。

Method: 1. 提出匹配原则：估计标签保持部署扰动的协方差，然后沿其范围正则化编码器Jacobian。2. 在线性高斯模型中给出闭式最优性证明（定理A），包括立方根注水填充、Jacobian惩罚的范围覆盖必要性（定理G）等7个引理和推论。3. 提出Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI)，一种无标签的嵌入敏感性探针。4. 在13项预注册实验中验证理论预测，涵盖经典ML到Qwen2.5-7B规模。

Result: 13项预注册实验中12项通过理论预测（matched > isotropic > wrong-W的排序）。唯一的例外（Office-31）在实验前已被标记为特征值间隙失败案例。在7B规模上，匹配风格的PMH方法改进了选择性诚实性，并保持了Style TDI，而标准DPO会使其退化。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于命名了部署扰动协方差这一核心对象，明确了正则化器必须满足的条件，并提供了一个闭式可证伪的理论框架。该理论的目的不是在所有排行榜上追求通用性，而是提供一个统一的分析视角。

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [129] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于持续三维重建和序列策略的纯好奇心驱动探索方法（ReCuriosity），通过在HM3D中纯好奇心训练，使智能体在零样本下泛化到Gibson和AI生成世界，并在下游任务中优于从头训练的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏奖励、长视距的3D环境中，好奇心驱动强化学习因智能体陷入局部循环（反复探索被遗忘的状态）而失效，根源在于缺乏空间持久性和情景上下文。

Method: 使用在线3D重建作为持续更新的持久世界模型，同时将策略参数化为基于RGB观测的序列模型以维护情景轨迹历史，实现纯好奇心驱动训练。

Result: 在HM3D中纯好奇心训练，零样本泛化到Gibson和AI生成世界；在下游任务（摘苹果、图像目标导航）中优于从头训练的基线。

Conclusion: 空间持久性（持续3D重建）和情景上下文（序列策略）是好奇心驱动探索在复杂3D环境中成功的关键，且学到的策略可高效迁移至下游任务。

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Polars inside Intel SGX2 Enclaves: An Empirical Study of Confidential Analytical Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21797)
*Wei Wang,Burns Smith,Kenny Leftin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Arrow-native的DataFrame引擎Polars在Intel SGX2安全飞地中的性能表现。在TPC-H SF30基准测试中，端到端开销保持在1.49-1.56×，但查询开销下降（1.51→1.43×）而表加载开销上升（2.27→4.07×）。中位单查询SGX减速为1.45×，最大达2.57×。惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27×，且后者在41GB以上内存不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE机密分析研究主要关注SQL数据库引擎或早期SGX版本，缺乏对Arrow-native DataFrame引擎在最新SGX2上的性能评估，特别是未能区分计算开销与数据摄入开销的差异。

Method: 在Intel SGX2飞地中通过Gramine运行Polars，使用TPC-H SF30基准测试和Azure Blob Storage，设计了标准功率测试和仅查询变体以分离开销来源，同时比较惰性与急切API的性能差异。

Result: 端到端开销1.49-1.56×近似恒定；查询开销从1.51-1.52×降至1.43-1.44×；表加载开销从2.27×升至4.07×；中位查询减速1.45×，最大2.57×；惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27×，急切API在41GB以上OOM。

Conclusion: SGX2可支持Arrow-native分析处理，安全开销与现有SQL方案同数量级，但加载路径放大和API级优化是端到端性能的一阶决定因素。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have renewed interest in confidential analytics, but most prior evaluations focus on SQL database engines or earlier SGX generations. This paper studies an Arrow-native DataFrame engine, Polars, running inside Intel SGX2 enclaves via Gramine on TPC-H SF30 with Azure Blob Storage. We report both the standard TPC-H power score and a query-only variant that removes table-loading time in order to separate compute overhead from data-ingestion overhead. Across four dataset-width configurations (approximately 22-73 GB), end-to-end overhead remains nearly constant at 1.49-1.56$\times$, but this composite metric obscures two distinct behaviors: query-only overhead declines from 1.51-1.52$\times$ to 1.43-1.44$\times$, whereas table-loading overhead rises from 2.27$\times$ to 4.07$\times$. We further show that overhead is not uniform across queries: for the len130 configuration, the median per-query SGX slowdown is 1.45$\times$ with a maximum of 2.57$\times$, and a small set of queries exhibits pronounced run-to-run spikes consistent with stateful EPC pressure. Finally, we compare Polars' lazy and eager APIs under the same TEE setting. Lazy execution is 2.25-2.27$\times$ faster overall, while eager execution fails with out-of-memory errors at 41 GB and above. Relative to the recent DuckDB-SGX2 study, our results suggest that SGX2 can support Arrow-native analytical processing with a similar order of security overhead, but that load-path amplification and API-level optimization are first-order determinants of end-to-end performance.

</details>


### [131] [Chain Reactions: How Nonce Collisions in ECDSA Compromise Polygon MEV Searchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21498)
*Yash Madhwal,Andrey Seoev,Raffaele Della Pietra,Anastasiia Smirnova,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了Polygon MEV生态系统中由于非ce重用导致的ECDSA私钥恢复漏洞，攻击者可通过线性方程组从链上签名中提取私钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易依赖ECDSA签名安全，但非ce生成不当会导致私钥泄露。Polygon MEV生态中搜索者为追求亚秒级响应时间而使用可预测的非ce模式，产生系统性安全风险。

Method: 分析链上数据识别非ce重用模式；建立线性方程组形式化攻击模型（包括跨钱包非ce碰撞场景）；利用初等代数从重复非ce的签名中恢复私钥。

Result: 在Polygon链上发现可被利用的非ce模式；证明协议引入的延迟压力（密封投标拍卖）是导致非ce重用的根本原因；单个实现错误可同时危及多个账户。

Conclusion: 协议层面的延迟压力会引发灾难性的密码学失效，区块链系统需对非ce生成实施更严格的密码学级安全约束。

Abstract: ECDSA signatures form the bedrock of blockchain transaction authentication, yet their security critically depends on proper nonce generation. We uncover a critical vulnerability in the Polygon MEV ecosystem: systematic nonce reuse that enables complete private key recovery. Analyzing on-chain data reveals that searchers, driven by the need for sub-second response times in sealed-bid auctions, employ predictable nonce patterns. These patterns create linear relationships between signatures, allowing passive attackers to recover private keys using elementary algebra. We provide a compact linear-system formulation for such attacks, including the dangerous case of cross-wallet nonce collisions, and present concrete evidence of exploitable patterns on Polygon. Our findings demonstrate how protocol-induced latency pressures can lead to catastrophic cryptographic failures in production blockchain systems, where a single implementation error compromises multiple accounts simultaneously.

</details>


### [132] [Frequency-Domain Regularized Adversarial Alignment for Transferable Attacks against Closed-Source MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21541)
*Leitao Yuan,Qinghua Mao,Daizong Liu,Kun Wang,Wenjie Wang,Yan Teng,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FRA-Attack通过统一的频域正则化方法（高频DCT特征对齐与模型无关的低通梯度正则化）提升多模态大模型跨模型迁移攻击的迁移性，在15个主流MLLM上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迁移的对抗攻击方法面临两个关键挑战：(1) 空间域特征冗余导致模型间共享的视觉关注点难以被有效捕捉；(2) 替代模型的梯度信号包含特定于该模型的行为信息，阻碍了跨模型迁移性。需要一种统一的方法来同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出FRA-Attack，包含两个核心组件：(1) 高频DCT目标函数——对patch特征应用高通DCT变换，抑制冗余全局结构，聚焦于承载MLLMs内在视觉关注的高频波段；(2) 频域梯度正则化(FGR)——一种模型无关的低通正则化器，仅利用几何频率坐标调制替代梯度，不依赖任何替代模型导出统计量，去除替代特定高频伪影同时保留可迁移的低频方向。

Result: 在7个厂商的15个旗舰MLLM上进行广泛实验，FRA-Attack实现了优越的跨模型迁移性，特别是在GPT-5.4、Claude-Opus-4.6和Gemini-3-flash上取得了最先进性能。

Conclusion: FRA-Attack通过统一的频域正则化视角，同时解决了特征对齐和梯度优化两个挑战，有效提升了对抗样本的跨模型迁移攻击能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain vulnerable to transfer-based targeted attacks, where perturbations optimized on open-source surrogate encoders can generalize to closed-source MLLMs. A key challenge for improving adversarial transferability is to effectively capture the intrinsic visual focus shared across different models, such that perturbations align with transferable semantic cues rather than surrogate-specific behaviors. However, existing methods suffer from spatial-domain feature redundancy and surrogate-specific gradient signals, thereby hindering cross-model transferability. In this paper, we propose FRA-Attack, which addresses both challenges from a unified frequency-domain regularization perspective. For feature alignment, a high-pass DCT objective on patch features suppresses redundant global structures and concentrates the loss on the high-frequency band that carries the MLLMs' intrinsic visual focus. For gradient optimization, we introduce Frequency-domain Gradient Regularization (FGR), a \textit{model-agnostic} low-pass regularizer that modulates the surrogate gradient using only the geometric frequency coordinate, \textit{i.e.}, no surrogate-derived statistic is involved, so that FGR is model-agnostic by construction, removing surrogate-specific high-frequency artifacts while preserving transferable low-frequency directions. Together, the two components form a unified frequency-domain treatment of transferability. Extensive experiments on $15$ flagship MLLMs across $7$ vendors show that FRA-Attack achieves superior cross-model transferability, particularly with state-of-the-art performance on GPT-5.4, Claude-Opus-4.6 and Gemini-3-flash.

</details>


### [133] [TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22709)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriSweep提出了一种四无人机编队架构，通过空间分集的电磁侧信道分析，在0.25-1.5米距离上对嵌入式微控制器进行自主远距攻击，利用相干合并和二阶掩码消除技术，在模拟环境中对ANSSI ASCAD数据集实现了有效密钥恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁侧信道分析假设探头静止且近距离，低估了空中对手的威胁。本文旨在探索利用无人机编队实现远距离自主电磁侧信道攻击的可行性。

Method: 设计四无人机编队：Anchor（全频谱采集）、Mask Probe（掩码寄存器加载泄露）、Cipher Probe（掩码SubBytes输出泄露），三架采集无人机将数据发送至Accumulator无人机进行相干合并（+4.8 dB SNR增益）和基于空间分离泄露流的中心化乘积二阶掩码消除。使用交叉相关对齐补偿无人机悬停振动。

Result: 在ATmega8515掩码AES-128的主数据集上，0.25米距离实现密钥排名18±1.7（五种子）。100样本抖动变体上，单无人机排名从89降至21。双通道CNN在Accumulator上收敛至损失0.454（随机基线5.545）。

Conclusion: 无人机编队远距EM-SCA在模拟环境中可行，下一步计划进行物理原型构建。空间分集策略能有效对抗掩码防护和时序抖动。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.

</details>


### [134] [ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY: A Cross-Build Binary Dataset with Temporal Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21615)
*Chang Liu,Noah Fleischmann,Nicolò Altamura,Edward Raff,James Holt,Kristopher Micinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY，一个包含73,610个二进制文件、涵盖248个开源项目、跨编译器/优化级别/平台/多历史版本的统一框架，将所有二进制文件的编译上下文、源代码、脆弱函数和包版本作为一等元数据存储，并通过三项分析验证其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制语料库仅能捕获一两个维度的二进制变异性（如跨编译器构建无时间轴，或含CVE标签的单构建二进制），缺乏同时包含跨构建多样性、跨版本历史以及CVE标签的可查询结构。

Method: 构建包含73,610个二进制文件的语料库，涵盖248个开源项目，使用GCC、Clang和MSVC在Linux和Windows上以多种优化级别编译，包含多年历史构建。每个二进制文件在数据库中被索引，链接到其源代码、函数、调试信息、变体构建、历史版本和脆弱函数。

Result: 通过三项分析验证：1) 三阶段LLM基准测试（识别、策略引导检测、跨构建迁移）测试LLM是推理漏洞还是模式匹配构建特定伪影；2) 比较MalConv嵌入、jTrans函数嵌入和TLSH模糊哈希，量化同版本包的聚类效果；3) 贝叶斯回归分解二进制相似性中时间距离、文件变更和提交次数的贡献。

Conclusion: ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY提供了一个统一的框架，整合了二进制多样性、版本历史和CVE标签，为二进制分析研究（尤其是漏洞检测和LLM推理研究）提供了丰富的基础设施支持。

Abstract: Existing binary corpora typically capture only one or two axes of binary variation: they either provide cross-compiler builds without a temporal axis, or CVE labels for single-build binaries. None combine cross-build diversity, cross-version history, and CVE labels into a queryable structure. We present ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY, which consolidates these dimensions into a unified framework where every binary's compilation context, source code, vulnerable functions, and package version are stored as first-class metadata.
  ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY comprises 73,610 binaries spanning 248 open-source projects, compiled across GCC, Clang, and MSVC at multiple optimization levels on Linux and Windows, with multi-year historical builds. Each binary is indexed in a database that links it to its source code, functions, debug info, variant builds, historical versions, and vulnerable functions. Three analyses demonstrate this structure's value: (1) a three-stage LLM benchmark (recognition, strategy-guided detection, and cross-build transfer) to test whether LLMs reason about binary vulnerabilities or pattern-match on build-specific artifacts; (2) a comparison of MalConv embeddings, jTrans function embeddings, and TLSH fuzzy hashes quantifying how same-package versions cluster in each space; and (3) a Bayesian regression decomposing binary similarity into contributions from temporal distance, file changes, and commits.

</details>


### [135] [Adversarial Reframing: A Framework for Targeted Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21674)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Swati Kar,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: THREAT是一个基于推理的多LLM协作框架，通过迭代搜索高效发现文本越狱提示，以更低的计算成本实现更高的攻击成功率，且被检测为有害的比例低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在现实场景中广泛部署，但仍易受基于提示的越狱攻击（jailbreaking）绕过安全过滤器，现有方法在效率和效果上存在不足。

Method: 将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题，提出THREAT框架协调多个LLM进行迭代搜索循环，通过推理驱动的对抗策略（重新框架与利用）高效寻找越狱提示。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型架构上，THREAT以更低计算成本取得更高攻击成功率；其生成的提示仅有不到1%被标记为有害，而对应的未修改提示约有50%被拒绝。

Conclusion: 揭示了已有对齐LLM中此前未被检测到的漏洞，THREAT可作为主动强化基础模型安全性的实用工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in diverse real-world settings, yet remain vulnerable to jailbreaking, where prompt-based attacks bypass safety filters. We present THREAT (Targeted Harmful generation via Reframing and Exploitation of Adversarial Tactics), a reasoning-driven framework that coordinates multiple LLMs in an iterative search loop to find textual jailbreak prompts. We formulate prompt discovery as a nonconvex optimization problem and provide an efficient solution that lowers runtime and improves attack effectiveness. Across diverse datasets and model architectures, THREAT delivers higher attack success rates with lower computational cost than prior methods. The crafted prompts were flagged as harmful in fewer than 1% of cases, compared with about 50% refusals for the corresponding unmodified prompts. These findings reveal previously undetected vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs and position THREAT as a practical tool for proactively strengthening the safety of foundation models.

</details>


### [136] [FuzzingBrain V2: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Vulnerability Discovery and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21779)
*Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Kewen Zhu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FuzzingBrain V2是一个基于多智能体的自动化漏洞检测系统，结合OSS-Fuzz实现可复现的漏洞分析，通过Suspicious Point控制流抽象实现最优粒度漏洞定位，采用逻辑驱动的分层函数分析和双层层叠模糊测试，以及基于MCP的静态+动态分析工具。在AIxCC 2025决赛数据集上达到90%检测率，并在真实部署中发现29个零日漏洞（含2个CVE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的漏洞检测方法存在三个关键挑战：LLM生成的漏洞报告误报率高且缺乏可复现验证；现有方法在漏洞定位粒度上存在两难（函数级粒度在上下文过多时会遗漏bug，行级粒度则缺乏足够上下文）；难以推理具有复杂跨函数依赖和触发条件的漏洞。

Method: (1) 基于Google OSS-Fuzz的全自动漏洞分析，确保所有报告的漏洞均可通过fuzzer复现；(2) Suspicious Point，一种新颖的基于控制流的抽象方法，实现最优粒度的精准漏洞定位；(3) 逻辑驱动的分层函数分析与双层层叠模糊测试，在资源约束下增强函数覆盖；(4) 基于MCP的静态和动态分析工具，结合上下文工程增强复杂漏洞推理能力。

Result: 在AIxCC 2025决赛C/C++数据集上达到90%检测率（检测出40个漏洞中的36个）；在真实世界部署中发现29个零日漏洞，涉及12个开源项目，所有漏洞均被维护者确认并修复，其中2个已分配CVE编号。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain V2通过结合模糊测试的可复现性与LLM的推理能力，在最优粒度上进行漏洞定位，并利用多智能体协作和上下文工程处理复杂跨函数漏洞，显著提升了自动化漏洞检测的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities pose critical security threats, with nearly 50,000 CVEs reported in 2025. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated vulnerability detection, three key challenges remain. First, LLM-generated vulnerability reports suffer from high false positive rates and lack
  reproducible verification. Second, existing LLM-based approaches use suboptimal granularities for vulnerability localization: function-level analysis overlooks bugs when context becomes extensive, while line-level analysis lacks sufficient context. Third, existing approaches have difficulty reasoning about
  vulnerabilities with complex cross-function dependencies and triggering conditions.
  We present FuzzingBrain V2, a multi-agent system that addresses these gaps through four key contributions: (1) fully automated vulnerability analysis built on Google's OSS-Fuzz, ensuring all reported vulnerabilities are fuzzer-reproducible; (2) Suspicious Point, a novel control-flow-based abstraction for precise
  vulnerability localization at the optimal granularity; (3) logic-driven hierarchical function analysis with dual-layer fuzzing enhancing function coverage under resource constraints; (4) MCP-based static and dynamic analysis tools with context engineering enhancing complex vulnerability reasoning.
  On the AIxCC 2025 Final Competition C/C++ dataset, FuzzingBrain V2 achieved 90% detection rate (36 of 40 vulnerabilities). In real-world deployment, FuzzingBrain V2 discovered 29 zero-day vulnerabilities across 12 open-source projects, all confirmed and fixed by maintainers, with 2 assigned CVE IDs.

</details>


### [137] [Graph Structure of Chebyshev Permutation Polynomials over Binary and Ternary Adic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21819)
*Xiaoxiong Lu,Yuling Dai,Chengqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了环 $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$ 上Chebyshev置换多项式的函数图结构，给出了路径长度和循环结构的显式刻画，证明了图具有强正则性，包括给定长度的循环数量恒定以及随 $k_1$ 和 $k_2$ 增长的可预测分支模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限域上非线性映射的函数图结构对于分析其动力学复杂性以及在密码学和伪随机生成中的潜在应用至关重要。

Method: 基于Chebyshev多项式模2和3幂次的新性质，将环中元素视为顶点，多项式定义的映射关系视为有向边，对函数图中的路径长度和循环结构进行显式刻画。

Result: 函数图展现出强正则性：给定长度的循环数量恒定，且随着 $k_1$ 和 $k_2$ 的增长存在可预测的分支模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果扩展了先前在素数幂环上的工作，为理解数字非线性映射中复杂性的涌现提供了见解，并支持了其密码学应用的安全性分析。

Abstract: Understanding the functional graph of a nonlinear map over a finite domain is crucial for analyzing its dynamical complexity and potential applications in cryptography and pseudorandom generation. In this paper, we investigate the graph structure of Chebyshev permutation polynomials over the ring $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$, where $k_1$ and $k_2$ are positive integers and $0\in\{k_1, k_2\}$. Each element of the ring is regarded as a vertex, and the mapping relation defined by the polynomial corresponds to a directed edge. Building on new properties of Chebyshev polynomials modulo powers of $2$ and $3$, we provide an explicit characterization of path lengths and cycle structures in the functional graph. We show that, despite the complexities introduced by the binary and ternary components, the graph exhibits strong regularities, including a constant number of cycles of a given length and predictable branching patterns as $k_1$ and $k_2$ increase. Our results extend previous studies over prime-power rings, offering insights into the emergence of complexity in digital nonlinear maps and supporting the security analysis of their cryptographic applications.

</details>


### [138] [A Large Language Model Approach to Generating Bypass Rules for Malware Evasion in Analysis Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21821)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABLE系统，利用大语言模型自动生成YARA规则以绕过沙箱逃逸检测，在334个真实恶意软件样本上达到79%的绕过成功率，相比现有平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沙箱逃逸检测依赖人工逆向分析每个逃逸机制来手动编写绕过规则，这种方法无法应对快速演变的逃逸技术，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: ABLE采用大语言模型分析因潜在逃逸行为而终止的恶意软件执行轨迹，运用多种推理策略生成针对性YARA绕过规则，并通过自动清理管道和反馈驱动的迭代精炼来修复语法错误并提升规则有效性。

Result: 在334个真实恶意软件样本上，ABLE在四种开源大语言模型上实现79%的绕过成功率，其中迭代精炼贡献了29.5%的成功案例；相比现有分析平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的自动规则生成方法可有效替代人工逆向分析的沙箱逃逸绕过方式，在可扩展性和效果上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Sandbox evasion remains a critical challenge for automated malware analysis, as modern malware employs environment checks to detect analysis platforms and suppress malicious behavior. Existing approaches rely on manually crafted bypass rules that require deep reverse engineering of each evasion mechanism -an approach that cannot scale against rapidly evolving evasion techniques. In this paper, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate YARA rules that bypass evasion checks in sandbox environments. We propose ABLE, which analyzes execution traces from malware terminated due to potentially evasive behavior and employs multiple reasoning strategies to generate targeted bypass rules. To address syntactic errors and improve the efficacy of the bypass rules in the LLM outputs, we introduce an auto-sanitization pipeline and feedback-driven iterative refinement. We evaluate ABLE on 334 real-world malware samples across four open-weight LLMs. ABLE achieves a 79% bypass success rate, with iterative refinement contributing 29.5% of successful cases. Compared to existing analysis platforms, ABLE identifies 47% more malware family classifications and exposes previously hidden behaviors.

</details>


### [139] [Quality-Assured Fuzz Harness Generation via the Four Principles Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21824)
*Ze Sheng,Dmitrijs Trizna,Luigino Camastra,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: QuartetFuzz是一个基于LLM的自动化测试桩生成系统，通过"四项原则"框架（逻辑正确性、API协议合规、安全边界尊重、入口点充分性）在生成阶段系统性地确保测试桩正确性。在23个开源项目上部署后，提交42个漏洞报告，29个被确认/修复（含3个CVE），误报率仅4.8%。作为质量审计工具，在586个现有测试桩中发现53个违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化测试桩生成工具无法系统性地确保测试桩正确性，逻辑错误、API误用和生命周期违规等缺陷在源码层面未被检测。随着LLM驱动的生成规模化，失控的质量将把规模化变成负担。

Method: 提出"四项原则"框架（P1-P4），首次从源码层面定义测试桩正确性并给出数学规范与可实现的检查。基于此构建自主LLM智能体，通过"生成-检查-修复"循环，在模糊测试开始前确保测试桩满足P1-P4要求。

Result: 在23个C/C++、Java和JavaScript开源项目上部署，提交42个漏洞报告，29个被确认/修复（含3个CVE），仅2个被拒绝（FP率4.8%）。内置P1/P2检查自动拦截58个测试桩导致的崩溃。审计586个生产测试桩发现53个违规（45个确认，35个已修复）。

Conclusion: QuartetFuzz通过系统化的正确性保障机制，有效提升了自动化测试桩生成的质量，降低了误报率，并在真实项目中取得了显著效果。验证了在LLM驱动的规模化生成中引入质量控制的必要性和可行性。

Abstract: Fuzz testing is the dominant technique for finding memory-safety vulnerabilities in C/C++ software, yet its effectiveness hinges on the quality of fuzz harnesses -- the programs that bridge fuzzers and library APIs. A growing body of tools now automate harness generation, but none systematically ensures the correctness of produced harnesses: logic errors, API misuse, and lifecycle violations go undetected at the source level. As LLM-driven generation scales harness creation, uncontrolled quality turns scale into a liability.
  We present QuartetFuzz, an autonomous harness-generation system that systematically improves correctness throughout the generation process. At its core is the Four Principles framework -- Logic Correctness (P1), API Protocol Compliance (P2), Security Boundary Respect (P3), and Entry Point Adequacy (P4) -- the first source-level definition of harness correctness with mathematical specifications and implementable checks. We operationalize these principles in an autonomous LLM agent that produces harnesses satisfying P1-P4 through a generate-check-fix loop before any fuzzing begins.
  Deployed on 23 open-source projects spanning C/C++, Java, and JavaScript, the system submits 42 bug reports, of which 29 are fixed or confirmed upstream (including 3 CVEs) and only 2 are rejected (4.8% FP rate). During generation, the built-in P1/P2 checks automatically intercepted 58 harness-induced crashes that would otherwise have been false positives. Applied as a quality auditor to 586 existing production harnesses across 70 projects, the system identifies 53 violations (45 confirmed, 35 fixed). We release a dataset of 100 labeled harnesses for reproducible evaluation. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/OwenSanzas/QuartetFuzz

</details>


### [140] [SPIDER: Two Server Functionality for the Cost of Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21857)
*Ofir Dvir,Kali Hale,Javin Zipkin,Divyakant Agrawal,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了baseSPIDER和SPIDER两种隐私信息检索方案。baseSPIDER在单服务器有状态客户端场景下实现了渐近最优通信复杂度并改进了常数因子，设计更为简洁。SPIDER是首个无需服务器特殊配合的单服务器PIR方案，通过简单转换baseSPIDER实现，消除了部署障碍，可即时应用于现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单服务器PIR方案在通信复杂度上存在常数因子优化空间，且大多需要服务器提供特殊API或协议特定交互，部署门槛高。本文旨在设计一种更简单高效且无需服务器配合的PIR方案，以降低实际部署障碍。

Method: baseSPIDER采用单服务器+有状态客户端架构，客户端通过预处理存储提示信息。SPIDER通过将baseSPIDER转换为默认服务器接口范式构建，该转换方法可推广至其他三种近期PIR方案。

Result: baseSPIDER达到了与最先进方案相同的渐近最优通信复杂度，并在常数因子上更优，尤其适合大条目数据库。SPIDER是首个无需服务器特殊API、辅助状态或协议特定交互的单服务器PIR方案。

Conclusion: SPIDER消除了PIR的部署障碍，可直接应用于现有系统。其设计更简单但客户端计算开销更高。提出的转换方法具有通用性，可适用于其他PIR方案。

Abstract: We introduce baseSPIDER and SPIDER, private information retrieval (PIR) schemes that embody two technical advancements. The baseSPIDER protocol operates with a single server and a stateful client that performs pre-processing and stores hints for future queries. In this setting, baseSPIDER introduces a new approach that matches the asymptotically optimal communication complexity of state-of-the-art schemes while improving constant factors--an advantage that is particularly significant for databases with large entries. In addition, baseSPIDER offers a conceptually simpler design relative to prior protocols. SPIDER operates over a default database interface and requires no cooperation from the server at any stage. To our knowledge, SPIDER is the first single-server PIR construction of this design, achieving privacy without specialized APIs, auxiliary server state, or protocol-specific interaction beyond conventional indexed access. SPIDER is built via a simple transformation of baseSPIDER to the default server setting, eliminating deployment barriers and enabling immediate applicability to existing systems. This transformation can be applied more broadly to three recent PIR solutions, adapting them for use in the default-server paradigm and yielding solutions of independent interest. SPIDER compares to the resulting modified solutions by exhibiting a simpler design while incurring higher client computational work.

</details>


### [141] [Blind Spots in the Guard: How Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks Evade Detection in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22001)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM智能体注入检测器在面对“领域伪装注入”（使用目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构生成的载荷）时存在系统性盲点，检测率从93.8%骤降至9.7%（Llama 3.1 8B）和从100%降至55.6%（Gemini 2.0 Flash），并形式化定义了伪装检测差距（CDG）。实验在45个任务、三个领域和两个模型家族上验证了CDG的显著性和一致性，生产级安全分类器Llama Guard 3的伪装注入检测率为零。多智能体辩论架构会放大静态注入攻击（小模型高达9.9倍），而针对性检测增强只能提供部分修复，表明该漏洞对弱模型是架构性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体注入检测器基于静态、模板化的载荷进行校准，但这些载荷明确声明自身为覆盖指令，无法检测使用目标文档领域词汇和权威结构生成的伪装注入载荷。

Method: 形式化定义了Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG)，即在静态注入与伪装注入之间检测率的差异；在45个任务、三个领域、两个模型家族（Llama 3.1 8B和Gemini 2.0 Flash）上进行系统性评估，并额外评估了生产级安全分类器Llama Guard 3；进一步分析了多智能体辩论架构对注入攻击的影响以及针对性检测增强的修复效果。

Result: 静态注入检测率从93.8%降至伪装注入的9.7%（Llama 3.1 8B），从100%降至55.6%（Gemini 2.0 Flash）。CDG在统计上显著（p<0.001），零反向不一致对。Llama Guard 3对伪装注入的检测率为0%。多智能体辩论架构对较小模型放大静态注入攻击高达9.9倍。针对性检测增强仅提供部分修复（Llama提升10.2%，Gemini提升78.7%）。

Conclusion: 领域伪装注入是LLM智能体安全的一个系统性盲点，对弱模型而言该漏洞是架构性的而非偶然的，需要更根本的安全设计改进。

Abstract: Injection detectors deployed to protect LLM agents are calibrated on static, template-based payloads that announce themselves as override directives. We identify a systematic blind spot: when payloads are generated to mimic the domain vocabulary and authority structures of the target document, what we call domain camouflaged injection, standard detectors fail to flag them, with detection rates dropping from 93.8% to 9.7% on Llama 3.1 8B and from 100% to 55.6% on Gemini 2.0 Flash. We formalize this as the Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG), the difference in injection detection rate between static and camouflaged payloads. Across 45 tasks spanning three domains and two model families, CDG is large and statistically significant (chi^2 = 38.03, p < 0.001 for Llama; chi^2 = 17.05, p < 0.001 for Gemini), with zero reverse discordant pairs in either case. We additionally evaluate Llama Guard 3, a production safety classifier, which detects zero camouflage payloads (IDRcamouflage = 0.000), confirming that the blind spot extends beyond few-shot detectors to dedicated safety classifiers. We further show that multi-agent debate architectures amplify static injection attacks by up to 9.9x on smaller models, while stronger models show collective resistance. Targeted detector augmentation provides only partial remediation (10.2% improvement on Llama, 78.7% on Gemini), suggesting the vulnerability is architectural rather than incidental for weaker models. Our framework, task bank, and payload generator are released publicly.

</details>


### [142] [Parser-Free Querying of Security Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22027)
*Evan Luo,Julien Piet,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sieve是一个利用大语言模型从自然语言安全问题生成可执行查询代码的系统，仅需一次LLM调用加确定性执行，在133个安全查询评估中比人工脚本减少3倍以上错误率，特别是在多行时间关联查询上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析师需要查询系统日志检测威胁和调查事件，但每种日志源使用不同的半结构化格式，标准方法需要为每种格式构建解析器，工程成本高；而直接使用grep等工具查询原始日志则缺乏表达多行时间查询的能力，无法满足安全调查需求。

Method: Sieve通过自动提取轻量级日志格式上下文来引导大语言模型，仅需一次LLM调用生成查询代码，随后进行确定性执行，无需多次迭代调用。

Result: 在5种日志类型的133个安全查询评估中，Sieve在复杂时间序列和跨事件查询上相比人工分析师脚本降低了3倍以上的错误率，在多行关联任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 实验证明LLM生成的代码能够弥合结构化日志查询的表达能力与直接处理原始文件的即时性之间的差距。
