Method: 提出一个概率算法，利用系统的可组合结构（f_i = h_i(g_1,...,g_n)），将原系统转化为关于g_j变量的简化系统，并计算所有孤立正则解。算法的算术复杂度在输入规模和解的数量上均为多项式级别。

Result: 算法能够高效计算所有孤立正则解，复杂度为关于输入规模和解数量的多项式。两个重要应用场景：一是f_i属于由代数无关多项式g_j生成的子环；二是有限反射群（如对称群S_n、超八面体群B_n、二面体群I_2(m)及例外反射群E_6,E_7,E_8,F_4,H_3,H_4）的不变多项式系统。

Conclusion: 利用可组合结构可以显著提升多项式系统求解效率，该算法在不变多项式系统（特别是有限反射群的不变环）中有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We study the problem of computing the isolated regular solutions of a system \((f_1,\ldots,f_n)\) of \(n\) polynomial equations in \(n\) variables \((X_1, \dots, X_n)\) over a field of characteristic zero \(k\). We focus on systems with a \emph{composable structure}, where each polynomial \(f_i\) can be expressed as a composition \( f_i = h_i(g_1,\dots,g_n)\). Exploiting this structure allows us to reduce the original system to one in the \(g_j\) variables, thereby significantly improving the efficiency of symbolic solution algorithms. We present a probabilistic algorithm that computes all isolated regular solutions, with arithmetic complexity being polynomial in the input size and in the number of solutions.
  A first important application is when \(f_1, \dots, f_n\) belong to the subring \(k[g_1, \dots, g_n]\), where \(g_1, \dots, g_n\) are algebraically independent polynomials in \(k[X_1, \dots, X_n]\). Another important application is to systems of invariant polynomials under finite reflection groups, since by the Chevalley-Shephard-Todd theorem their invariant rings are polynomial algebras. Typical examples include the symmetric groups \(S_n\), the hyperoctahedral groups \(B_n\), the dihedral groups \(I_2(m)\), and the exceptional finite reflection groups \(E_6, E_7, E_8, F_4, H_3, H_4\).

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Contract Based Verification of Non-functional Requirements for Embedded Automotive C Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21532)
*Jesper Amilon,Merlijn Sevenhuijsen,Mattias Nyberg,Karl Palmskog*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对C语言模块的接口规范合约语言，用于检查安全关键嵌入式系统中非功能需求（如控制流和数据流限制），并实现了Frama-C插件，通过两个斯堪尼亚卡车案例研究验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ACSL合约只能验证功能需求，无法检查非功能需求（如禁止调用不安全函数、未初始化变量等），而MISRA-C等规则集是正交的。对于来自分包商、初级开发者或LLM生成的不可信代码，需要额外的约束检查机制。

Method: 构建了一套面向C语言模块非功能需求的通用规则集，提出模块接口规范合约语言，并实现为Frama-C插件。该插件以C模块及其合约为输入，检查控制流和数据流属性（如仅允许调用特定函数）。

Result: 在斯堪尼亚卡车的两个安全关键C代码案例中，基于非正式系统需求定义了模块合约和ACSL函数合约，并通过工具链完成了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的模块合约语言和检查工具能有效补充现有功能验证框架，确保安全关键系统中不可信代码满足非功能需求。

Abstract: Code contracts provide a robust way to specify functional requirements of safety-critical software in embedded systems. For example, the ANSI/ISO C Specification Language (ACSL) can be used to specify the functional behavior of C code that is then formally verified by the Frama-C framework's Wp plugin. However, non-functional requirements, such as restrictions on control flow and data flow, are also important for embedded systems safety. Untrusted code developed by subcontractors, junior developers, or generated by large language models, can be verified by Wp but may nevertheless call unsafe functions or use uninitialized program variables. To address this problem, we constructed a set of general rules concerning non-functional requirements of C code in safety-critical embedded systems. Our rules are orthogonal to popular C rulesets such as MISRA-C and center on modules and their interaction through interfaces. To enable checking our rules, we propose an interface specification contract language for C modules. We implemented a checker for our rules as a Frama-C plugin, which takes as input a C module and its contract and checks control flow and data flow properties, ensuring, e.g., that only permitted functions are called by the module. We integrated our checker in a toolchain to enable specification and verification of module contracts and ACSL contracts for untrusted code. We report on two case studies on safety-critical C code using software in Scania trucks, where we defined module contracts and ACSL function contracts based on informal system requirements and verified them using our toolchain.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Quantum Genetic Optimization for Negative Selection Algorithms in Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22527)
*Giancarlo P. Gamberi,Calebe P. Bianchini*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出量子遗传负选择算法(QGNSA)，通过将量子遗传算法融入EvoSeedRNSA以替代经典进化优化过程，利用量子叠加和概率幅调整增强探测器生成效率，在元宇宙金融交易数据集上表现优于经典方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典负选择算法中探测器生成的效率是制约其异常检测性能的关键瓶颈，现有方法在搜索空间探索和收敛效率方面存在不足。

Method: 将量子遗传算法(QGA)集成到EvoSeedRNSA框架中，利用量子叠加态和概率幅调整机制替代经典的进化优化过程，以增强探测器生成阶段的搜索空间探索能力和收敛效率。

Result: 在元宇宙金融交易数据集上的实验表明，QGNSA相比经典方法取得了更优的异常检测准确率，并在不同超参数配置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 量子计算在人工智能免疫系统特别是高维异常检测任务中具有潜在优势；未来工作包括优化量子电路设计、在真实量子硬件上部署以及探索混合量子-经典方法。

Abstract: Negative Selection Algorithms (NSAs), inspired by the self/non-self discrimination mechanism of the human immune system, have been widely employed in anomaly detection. However, their effectiveness is often constrained by the efficiency of detector generation. This paper presents the Quantum Genetic Negative Selection Algorithm (QGNSA), a novel approach that integrates a Quantum Genetic Algorithm (QGA) into the EvoSeedRNSA algorithm, replacing its classical evolutionary optimization process. The proposed method exploits quantum superposition and probabilistic amplitude adjustment to enhance search space exploration and convergence efficiency in the detector generation process. Empirical evaluations using the Metaverse Financial Transactions Dataset demonstrate that QGNSA achieves superior anomaly detection accuracy compared to its classical counterpart while maintaining robustness under varying hyperparameter configurations. The experimental results highlight the potential advantages of quantum computing in artificial immune systems, particularly in high-dimensional anomaly detection tasks. Future research will focus on further optimizing quantum circuit design, deploying the algorithm on real quantum hardware, and exploring hybrid quantum-classical approaches for improved computational efficiency.

</details>


### [180] [Guiding Multi-Objective Genetic Programming with Description Length Improves Symbolic Regression Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22374)
*Gabriel Kronberger,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca,Deaglan J. Bartlett,Harry Desmond,Pedro G. Ferreira*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文评估了描述长度（DL）和分数贝叶斯因子（FBF）作为遗传规划符号回归中防止过拟合和结构膨胀的模型选择准则，发现DL/FBF后选择优于AIC/BIC基线，但直接作为适应度函数会导致过早收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传规划符号回归（GPSR）在存在噪声时容易过拟合和结构膨胀，需要更鲁棒、数据高效的模型选择方法。

Method: 将DL（基于Fisher信息的参数编码）和FBF与AIC、BIC进行对比，在含噪合成基准和真实回归数据集上测试三种搜索/选择策略：多目标搜索+DL/FBF后选择、DL直接作为多目标搜索目标、DL/FBF作为单目标适应度函数。

Result: DL/FBF后选择相比AIC/BIC基线提升了测试性能；BIC结合DL/FBF的函数复杂度惩罚可产生类似结果。将DL/FBF直接作为单目标适应度函数时，易导致早熟收敛于过于简单的模型。

Conclusion: DL/FBF可作为遗传规划工作流中鲁棒的模型选择工具，提供了将其有效集成到遗传规划工作流中的实用指导。

Abstract: Symbolic regression with genetic programming (GPSR) may suffer from overfitting and structural bloat, especially when noise is present. In this paper we evaluate description length (DL) and fractional Bayes factor (FBF) criteria as principled, data-efficient alternatives to heuristics for selecting compact expressions that generalise well. We implement DL using a Fisher-information-based parameter encoding and compare it to AIC and BIC across multiple datasets, including noisy synthetic benchmarks and real-world regression problems. We study three search/selection strategies: (i) multi-objective search for accuracy and program length followed by DL/FBF selection; (ii) multi-objective search using DL directly as an objective; and (iii) single-objective optimisation with DL/FBF as the fitness. Across datasets we find that DL/FBF post-selection improves test performance compared to AIC/BIC baseline and that BIC in combination with the same function complexity penalty from DL/FBF produces similar results. In contrast, using DL/FBF directly as a fitness function in single-objective GPSR frequently induces premature convergence to overly simple models. We conclude with practical guidance for using DL/FBF as robust model-selection tools in genetic programming workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Impact of Atmospheric Turbulence and Pointing Error on Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22268)
*Celia Sánchez-de-Miguel,Antonio M. Mercado-Martínez,Beatriz Soret,Antonio Jurado-Navas,Miguel Castillo-Vázquez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强型图像模拟器，能够将垂直路径大气湍流和卫星指向抖动引入地球观测图像，并基于YOLOv8和RetinaNet评估了船舶检测性能，发现湍流和抖动会显著降低检测精度，其中RetinaNet表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI训练数据集很少考虑大气湍流和指向抖动等真实物理退化效应，导致模型在实际运行环境中性能不可靠，因此需要构建能生成物理逼真退化图像的模拟器来评估和改进模型鲁棒性。

Method: 基于先前工作，开发增强型图像模拟器，可模拟垂直路径大气湍流和由平台与传感器振动引起的卫星指向抖动，生成物理逼真的失真图像。以船舶检测为案例，使用YOLOv8和RetinaNet在不同湍流和指向误差水平下进行评估。

Result: YOLOv8的召回率在理想条件下为91%，弱湍流下降至60%，强湍流或抖动下低于40%；RetinaNet在退化条件下保持约75%的召回率，表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在EO训练数据集中纳入真实的物理退化效应对于确保AI模型在实际运行环境中的可靠性至关重要，海上监视应用验证了这一结论。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) imagery is often degraded by atmospheric turbulence and pointing jitter; yet, these effects are rarely considered in datasets used to train AI-based detection models. Based on prior work, this paper presents an enhanced image simulator that enables the incorporation of vertical-path atmospheric turbulence and satellite pointing jitter, arising from platform and sensor vibrations, to generate physically realistic distorted images. As a case study, vessel detection is evaluated using YOLOv8 and RetinaNet on images generated by the proposed simulator under different levels of turbulence and pointing errors. Results show that YOLOv8 recall decreases from 91% under ideal conditions to 60% in the presence of weak turbulence, and falls below 40% under strong turbulence or jitter. In contrast, RetinaNet demonstrates greater robustness, maintaining approximately 75% recall across degraded conditions. These results highlight the importance of incorporating realistic physical degradations into EO training datasets to ensure reliable performance of AI-based models in operational environments, as demonstrated in maritime surveillance applications.

</details>


### [182] [Throughput and Delay Performance of Slotted Aloha in SmartBANs under Saturation Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22317)
*Anastasios C. Politis,Constantinos S. Hilas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对ETSI SmartBAN规范定义的slotted Aloha协议，在饱和条件下建立了二维离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）模型，评估其饱和吞吐量和平均端到端延迟，并通过仿真验证了理论模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估ETSI SmartBAN规范中slotted Aloha协议在饱和条件下的性能，为该协议的预测和优化提供理论支持。

Method: 建立二维离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）模型，对协议的操作细节进行建模，推导饱和吞吐量和平均端到端延迟的理论表达式，并通过仿真验证模型精度。

Result: 理论模型与仿真结果高度吻合，证明了所提DTMC模型的准确性。

Conclusion: 所建模型可用于SmartBAN协议的性能预测与优化。

Abstract: This letter evaluates the performance of the slotted Aloha protocol defined by the European Telecommunication Standard Institute (ETSI) SmartBAN specification, under saturation conditions. For this purpose, we develop a two-dimensional Discrete Time Markov Chain (DTMC) to model the operational details of the protocol and assess its performance in terms of saturation throughput and average end-to-end delay. The accuracy of the proposed model is validated by means of simulation which reveals a very good match among theoretical and simulation results. The model can be used for protocol performance prediction and optimization purposes.

</details>


### [183] [SCALE: Sensitivity-Aware Federated Unlearning with Information Freshness Optimization for Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22589)
*Zihao Ding,Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SCALE框架，通过历史贡献分析与信息新鲜度感知的自适应稀疏化，实现联邦学习中更精确、高效的数据遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法存在遗忘精度低、缺乏时序信息反映的问题，导致遗忘效果次优，无法有效应对移动边缘计算中数据污染和隐私合规需求。

Method: SCALE采用双层遗忘框架：1）基于历史贡献的层敏感性分析，识别受目标客户端影响最大的层；2）在权重子组级别进行自适应稀疏化，平衡信息新鲜度与遗忘效果。

Result: 理论分析证明了收敛性和加速优势，实验与测试床结果相比现有基线方法在遗忘效果上有显著提升。

Conclusion: SCALE通过融合历史贡献分析与信息新鲜度感知的稀疏化，有效提升了联邦遗忘的精度和效率。

Abstract: Federated Unlearning (FU) is emerging as a powerful tool that enables the selective removal of client data to effectively address data contamination and meet strict privacy regulations in mobile edge computing (MEC) systems. Although FU has recently drawn attention in the AI community, existing approaches suffer from low unlearning precision and lack temporal information reflection, which results in suboptimal forgetting performance. To address these issues, we propose SCALE, a dual-level unlearning framework combining historical contribution analysis with information freshness-aware adaptive sparsification. Our framework first employs a historical contribution-based layer sensitivity analysis to identify layers most influenced by target clients, then performs fine-grained unlearning through adaptive sparsification at the weight sub-group level to balance information freshness with forgetting effectiveness. Through theoretical analysis, the proposed framework demonstrates the convergence properties and acceleration advantages. Our experiments and testbed results demonstrate superior unlearning effectiveness compared to state-of-the-art baselines, with significantly improved forgetting performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Flying Together: Human-Guided Immersive Shared Control for Aerial Robot Teams in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21680)
*Lou De Bel-Air,Luca Morando,Ruitao Chen,Keru Wang,Benjamin Jarvis,Charbel Toumieh,Yang Zhou,Ken Perlin,Dario Floreano,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于虚拟现实的共享控制框架，允许操作员通过运动基元规划器和导纳控制器实时引导多无人机编队在未知受限环境中导航，结合迁移点交互和视觉反馈，实验证明该方法能提升避障能力、保持编队间距并降低操作员负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主多机器人在未知非结构化环境中难以适应突发状况并捕捉操作员意图，现有方法缺乏灵活的人机协同机制。

Method: 设计了基于运动基元的用户引导规划器，结合导纳控制器实现操作员对无人机编队的柔性干预；通过VR中的迁移点交互和双边反馈界面实现人机协同导航。

Result: 共享控制显著提升了避障性能、维持了编队间距并降低了操作员的操作负担。

Conclusion: 沉浸式人在环中的多机器人导航具有可行性和优势，为未来人机协同的无人机编队控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While autonomous multi-robots can achieve safe and coordinated navigation, they often struggle to adapt to unforeseen conditions and to capture operator-driven objectives in unstructured environments. We present a Virtual Reality (VR)-based shared control framework for teams of drones operating in constrained and unknown environments, enabling real-time, user-guided exploration. At the core of our approach is a novel, user-guided motion-primitive-based planner that computes continuous, collision-free trajectories while continuously integrating operator input. This planner is coupled with an admittance controller, allowing the operator to flexibly influence team behavior and guide drones toward regions of interest that autonomous planners may overlook. The system supports mixed-reality operations with both physical and simulated drones, and implements a bilateral VR-based interface, allowing the operator to guide the robot team via migration points while receiving immediate visual feedback of the team state. Experimental results show that shared control improves obstacle avoidance, maintains inter-agent spacing, and reduces operator effort, demonstrating the feasibility and advantages of immersive, human-in-the-loop multi-robot navigation.

</details>


### [185] [Closed-Loop Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Deformable Microfiber Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21688)
*Alessandro Amici,Houari Bettahar,Veeti Jaakkola,Quan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种闭环仿真到现实的强化学习方法，在简化无摩擦仿真器中训练策略，直接迁移至40Hz物理双夹持器微操作系统中，无需重新训练或域适应，实现对表面微纤维的亚毫米级形状控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微尺度下表面和界面相互作用难以精确建模，限制了传统基于模型的控制和仿真到现实学习方法的应用，亟需一种能处理未建模表面交互的微操作控制方法。

Method: 在简化无摩擦仿真器中训练几何形状调节的强化学习策略，利用实时视觉反馈在部署时迭代校正未建模表面交互的影响，直接将策略迁移至物理系统。

Result: 在24种不同初始配置下，策略实现270±80μm的平均点状形状误差；在9个不同纤维直径(50/80/120μm)和操作长度(10/15/20mm)组合下，均实现亚毫米级最终形状误差，无需重新训练或调参。

Conclusion: 在简化仿真器中学习的策略，只要仿真到现实差距中任务相关效应在闭环反馈中保持可观测和可校正，就能实现可重复的真实世界微纤维形状调节。

Abstract: Autonomous contact-based micromanipulation is challenging because surface and interfacial interactions at the microscale are difficult to model accurately, limiting the use of conventional model-based control and sim-to-real learning. We present a closed-loop sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) approach for microfiber shape control on a surface. The central idea is to train geometric shape regulation in a simplified frictionless simulator and rely on real-time visual feedback during deployment to iteratively correct the observed effects of unmodeled surface interactions. An RL policy trained entirely in simulation is transferred directly to a physical dual-gripper micromanipulation system operating at 40 Hz, without retraining or domain adaptation. Using silk microfibers as a testbed, the policy achieves a mean point-wise shape error of 270 $\pm$ 80 $μ$m across twenty-four diverse initial configurations. Across nine specimens covering all combinations of three fiber diameters (50, 80, and 120 $μ$m) and three manipulated lengths (10 mm, 15mm, and 20 mm), the same policy achieves sub-millimeter final shape error without any retraining or retuning. These results show that a policy learned in a simplified simulator can achieve repeatable real-world microfiber shape regulation under surface contact, provided that the task-relevant effects of the sim-to-real mismatch remain observable and correctable within the closed feedback loop.

</details>


### [186] [Distributed Multi-Coverage for Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21686)
*Mariem Guitouni,Aaron T. Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分布式多覆盖算法，使无人机群在局部感知、局部通信且无全局协调的条件下，仍能保障关键资产的多重冗余覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中无人机群面临通信范围有限、机载计算能力受限、局部故障不能导致任务终止等约束，现有集中式整数规划方法虽能最优求解但无法满足分布式需求。

Method: 提出一种分布式多覆盖算法，机器人仅依靠局部感知和局部通信，无需全局协调即可实现多覆盖。

Result: 该算法能在分布式条件下实现多覆盖，适应通信范围有限、机载计算受限、部分系统故障不中断任务的实际部署需求。

Conclusion: 所提分布式算法有效解决了实际部署中机器人集群多覆盖任务的分布式协同问题，兼顾了冗余覆盖与系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous drone swarms deployed for surveillance, environmental monitoring, and infrastructure inspection must maintain reliable coverage of critical assets despite robot failures. This requires multicoverage: each asset must be observed by multiple robots for redundancy, with coverage requirements varying by asset importance. While recent work has solved the centralized problem optimally using integer programming, practical deployments face constraints that demand distributed solutions: robots operate with limited communication ranges, onboard computation restricts global planning, and partial system failures must not cause mission abort. We present a distributed multicoverage algorithm for robot swarms operating with local sensing, local communication, and no global coordination.

</details>


### [187] [Motion Design for Grasp-Based Dynamic Locomotion in Microgravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21704)
*Chaerim Moon,Joohyung Kim,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多肢体机器人在微重力环境下基于抓握的动态 locomotion 问题，提出了一个可参数化的运动规划框架，通过调节步态模式、步长、运动速度和名义姿态等参数，评估两种四足形态的稳定性和驱动力需求。结果表明，扩大可行接触力旋量空间和减弱冲击性全身动力学可提升运动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力环境下的移动通常依赖稀疏且不规则排列的锚点，这促使采用多肢体抓握式移动方式。然而，在耦合动态与运动学约束下，只有通过精心调节锚点交互和全身协调才能实现动态 locomotion，因此需要系统性地研究设计参数对运动性能的影响。

Method: 提出一个可参数化的运动规划框架，支持调节步态模式、步长、运动速度、名义姿态等参数；采用两种代表性四足形态在物理仿真中进行评估，以可行接触力旋量空间和全身冲击动力学作为性能指标。

Result: 扩大可行接触力旋量空间和减弱冲击性全身动力学能够有效提升微重力环境下的抓握式动态 locomotion 性能。

Conclusion: 这些发现为多肢体系统在微重力环境下的接触配置选择和全身协调策略提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Locomotion in microgravity often relies on sparsely and irregularly arranged anchors, motivating grasp-based mobility with multiple limbs. In this setting, dynamic locomotion is feasible only through deliberate regulation of both anchored interactions and whole-body coordination under coupled dynamic and kinematic constraints. This paper presents design insights for grasp-based dynamic locomotion with multi-limbed robotic systems in microgravity, targeting scenarios that require 6D limb manipulation to establish contacts with candidate anchors. The investigated design parameters include gait pattern, stride length, locomotion speed, and nominal posture. A parameterizable locomotion planning framework is proposed to support variations of these parameters and to evaluate the resulting locomotion performance in terms of stability and actuation demand. Two representative quadruped morphologies are adopted for evaluation in physics-based simulation. The results demonstrate that enlarging the feasible contact wrench space and attenuating impulsive whole-body dynamics improve locomotion performance. These findings inform strategies for contact configuration selection and whole-body coordination in microgravity locomotion with multi-limbed systems.

</details>


### [188] [Mind the Gaps: Multi-Robot Feedback-Driven Ergodic Coverage in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21719)
*Thales Costa Silva,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应覆盖策略，通过在线更新的环境参数模型构建目标空间信息分布，增强传统遍历轨迹优化，使多机器人团队能够在未知先验分布条件下动态优先覆盖高兴趣区域，并通过仿真验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遍历搜索方法需要预设目标分布，无法处理环境未知先验分布的场景，限制了多机器人自适应覆盖的效率。

Method: 提出自适应覆盖策略，利用环境模型的在线实时反馈，基于参数化模型构建目标空间信息分布，并将其融入传统遍历轨迹优化框架中，使机器人动态调整采样行为。

Result: 通过仿真验证了该方法在覆盖效率和资源分配方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能在未知先验分布条件下提升多机器人覆盖效率，优化个体控制策略与资源配置，适用于静态或缓变环境。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of multi-robot adaptive coverage, where teams of robots perform dynamic sampling by continuously adjusting their positions to collect data in an environment. This task can be challenging, particularly when robots must be efficiently allocated to new sampling locations over time. Ergodic search methods optimize robot trajectories by ensuring that the robots' time-averaged spatial distribution aligns with the spatial distribution of environmental information. While these methods promote effective exploration provided a target distribution, they often fail to account for unknown prior distributions of the environment. To overcome this limitation, we propose an adaptive coverage strategy that utilizes real-time feedback from an environmental model to adjust robot sampling behavior in response to unknown conditions. Our approach enhances traditional ergodic trajectory optimization by constructing a target spatial information distribution based on parametric models of the environment, which are updated online. This strategy assumes that the environment is either static or changes slowly compared to the robot's motion. Our framework allows robots to dynamically prioritize regions of high interest, improving coverage efficiency, synthesizing effective control policies for individual agents, and optimizing resource use in settings with unknown prior distributions. We validate our approach through simulations, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing coverage and resource allocation.

</details>


### [189] [PGDG: Physically Grounded Data Generation for Robust Bimanual Policy Learning from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21710)
*Cunxi Dai,Haoran Chang,Aditya Nisal,Rahul Kumar,Guofei Chen,Tao Chen,Yuzhe Qin,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PGDG是一种零样本数据生成框架，能将单条演示扩展为包含多样化物理可行恢复行为的数据集，显著提升双手操控任务的行为克隆性能，在仿真和真实世界中将成功率从约35-46%提升至77-93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的双手操控行为克隆面临两个挑战：多样化演示数据收集成本高，且微小扰动会使系统进入无恢复监督的流形外状态。

Method: PGDG通过物理接地采样器与数据集策展器之间的迭代循环工作：策展器选择信息丰富、非冗余且可恢复的行为来更新采样分布，使其覆盖欠采样的恢复模式；采样器从更新后的分布中抽取物理可行的轨迹候选并保留成功轨迹。为进一步提升数据质量，PGDG还采用短时域采样控制对选中的高风险状态重新标注修正动作。

Result: 在四项双手操控任务中，PGDG在仿真和零样本真实世界迁移中均优于纯空间增强方法。RotateBox-Pitch任务仿真成功率从38%提升至93%，真实世界从35%提升至82%。GR00T基础模型微调成功率从46%提升至77%。

Conclusion: PGDG能够从单条演示有效生成多样化恢复行为数据集，显著提升双手操控任务的行为克隆性能，并支持基础模型的高效微调。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for contact-rich bimanual manipulation remains challenging because diverse demonstrations are expensive to collect, and even small disturbances can push the system into off-manifold states where no recovery supervision is available. We propose PGDG, a data generation framework with zero-shot curation that expands a single demonstration into a compact dataset of physically plausible, successful, and diverse recovery behaviors without additional human labeling. PGDG iterates between a physics-grounded sampler and a dataset curator, where the curator selects informative, non-redundant, and recoverable behaviors to update the sampling distribution toward under-covered recovery modes, and the sampler draws physically plausible rollout candidates from this updated distribution and retains successful trajectories. To further improve data quality, PGDG applies short-horizon sampling-based control to relabel selected risky states with corrective actions. Across four bimanual manipulation tasks, PGDG consistently outperforms spatial-only augmentation in both simulation and zero-shot real-world transfer. On RotateBox-Pitch, success improves from 38% to 93% in simulation and from 35% to 82% in the real world. PGDG also enables effective foundation models fine-tuning such as GR00T, increasing success from 46% to 77%. Additional results are available in our website: https://cunxid.github.io/PGDG/.

</details>


### [190] [Safe and Steerable Geometric Motion Policies for Robotic Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21811)
*Albert Wu,Riccardo Bonalli,Thomas Lew,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafePBDS是一个几何一致的框架，通过将任务流形上的安全约束转化为配置流形加速度的线性约束，并结合任务流形动作接口，实现了机器人灵巧操作中的最优、可证明安全的加速控制。在23-DOF平台上，该方法在灵巧抓取任务中达到92.5%的成功率，并首次实现了超过360°的掌心朝下手中重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧操作需要在异构几何空间（如机器人的R^7配置流形、末端执行器的SE(3)位姿空间、障碍物避碰的R空间）上同时协调目标和约束，现有方法缺乏几何一致的统一框架来处理这种多空间约束下的安全控制问题。

Method: SafePBDS包含两个核心创新：(1) 拉回控制障碍函数构造——将任务流形上的安全条件转化为配置流形加速度的线性约束；(2) 任务流形动作接口——允许高层策略注入低维残差运动，零输入时恢复自主行为，任意输入下安全性得到保持。

Result: 在23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand平台上，灵巧抓取任务在20个家庭用品、120次试验中达到92.5%的成功率；通过一维动作接口排除任意一根手指，3指抓取在3个物体36次试验中达到94.4%的成功率；首次实现基于模型的全驱动掌心朝下手中重定向，在变化物体重量和手腕运动下实现超过360°的双向偏航旋转。

Conclusion: SafePBDS提供了一个几何一致的安全控制框架，有效解决了异构几何空间中的约束协调问题，其动作接口实现了高层策略的高效探索与底层精确运动的解耦，在灵巧抓取和手中重定向任务中均取得了优异性能。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous manipulation requires continuously reconciling objectives and constraints defined on heterogeneous geometric spaces: a robot controlled on a $\mathbb{R}^7$ configuration manifold may need to track end effector poses on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ while satisfying obstacle avoidance margins in $\mathbb{R}$. We present Safe Pullback Bundle Dynamical Systems (SafePBDS), a geometrically consistent framework that computes optimal, certifiably safe configuration manifold accelerations from objectives and safety requirements on arbitrary task manifolds. SafePBDS builds on prior work that combines predefined task manifold dynamical systems to produce autonomous motion. Its first innovation is a pullback control barrier function construction, which converts task manifold safety conditions into linear constraints on configuration manifold accelerations. The second innovation is a task manifold action interface that allows a high-level policy to inject low dimensional residual motions; zero input recovers the autonomous behavior, while safety is preserved under arbitrary inputs. This lets high-level policies efficiently steer exploration while leaving precise motion to the autonomous behavior. We validate SafePBDS in simulation and on a 23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand platform. On dexterous grasping, SafePBDS achieves a $92.5\%$ success rate across 20 household objects and 120 trials. Using the action interface, the method can exclude any one of the four fingers during grasping via a one-dimensional action, achieving $94.4\%$ 3-finger grasp success across 3 objects and 36 trials. The efficient planning and safety guarantee of SafePBDS also enables the first model-based, fully actuated palm-down in-hand reorientation, exceeding $360^\circ$ of yaw rotation in both directions under varying object weight and wrist motion. Demo video and details: https://tml.stanford.edu/safe-pbds

</details>


### [191] [Analytical and Experimental Force Analysis of a Soft Linear Pneumatic Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21836)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对线性软袖套致动器（LSSA）开展了理论与实验力分析，建立了准静态解析模型，揭示了压力、几何、位移、负载和轴向刚度耦合作用下的力产生机制，并通过实验验证了模型有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软袖套致动器（SSA）在可穿戴和辅助机器人系统中具有潜力，但其力产生行为尚未得到充分解释，特别是输出力随延伸的变化、外部负载的影响以及轴向刚度的力学作用。

Method: 建立了准静态解析模型，将净轴向力表示为来自端盖和折叠壁的压力贡献减去轴向刚度相关的力；模型包含内部压力、投影压力面积、折叠壁几何形状、轴向位移以及实验拟合的轴向刚度关系；通过定延伸和静负载实验评估致动器响应。

Result: 在125 kPa压力下，产生的力从零延伸时的约112 N下降到40 mm延伸时的接近零；静负载延迟了可测量的力产生并降低了力输出，尤其在低中压时；LSSA力产生由压力、几何、位移、负载和轴向刚度的耦合效应决定。

Conclusion: 所提出的解析模型能够有效描述LSSA的力-位移-压力关系，轴向刚度是影响力输出的关键因素，外部负载会显著改变致动器的力特性，为SSA的优化设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Soft sleeve actuators (SSAs) have recently been developed as a pneumatic actuation approach for wearable and assistive robotic systems. By integrating the actuation structure into a sleeve-like geometry, these actuators can reduce reliance on external attachment layers and transmission mechanisms while maintaining compliance with limb-shaped surfaces. However, the force-generation behavior of SSAs remains insufficiently explained, particularly with respect to the variation of output force during extension, the influence of external loading, and the mechanical role of axial stiffness. This paper presents an analytical and experimental force analysis of a linear soft sleeve actuator (LSSA). A quasi-static analytical model was developed by expressing the net axial force as the pressure-generated contribution from the cap and folded walls, reduced by the force associated with axial stiffness. The model incorporates internal pressure, projected pressure areas, folded wall geometry, axial displacement, and an experimentally fitted axial stiffness relation. Prescribed-extension and static-load experiments were conducted to evaluate the actuator response. At 125 kPa, the generated force decreased from approximately 112 N at zero extension to nearly zero at 40 mm. Static loading delayed measurable force generation and reduced force output, particularly at low and intermediate pressures. The results show that LSSA force generation is governed by coupled effects of pressure, geometry, displacement, loading, and axial stiffness.

</details>


### [192] [Superhuman Safe and Agile Racing through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22748)
*Ismail Geles,Leonard Bauersfeld,Markus Wulfmeier,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了多智能体强化学习（MARL）通过联赛自对弈训练无人机竞速，能够在超22m/s的高速下超越人类冠军飞行员，同时相比单智能体基线降低50%碰撞率，并零样本泛化到安全的人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主系统在隔离或仿真中表现超人类，但在真实共享动态空间中仍显脆弱，根本原因在于占主导地位的单智能体范式将其他参与者视为环境噪声，无法实现有效协调。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习，以高速四旋翼无人机竞速为高风险测试平台，通过联赛式自对弈训练智能体处理可变数量的竞速者，学习复杂气动交互、策略机动、主动避撞、超车及多智能体物理交互（如下洗气流）。

Result: 智能体在超22m/s的多机竞速中超越人类冠军飞行员，同时相比最先进的单智能体基线降低50%碰撞率；使用多样化人工智能体训练可实现零样本泛化到更安全的人机交互。

Conclusion: 实现鲁棒的机器人共存之路不在于孤立的单体安全约束，而在于多智能体交互的严格要求。

Abstract: Autonomous systems have achieved superhuman performance in isolation or simulation, yet they remain brittle in shared, dynamic real-world spaces. This failure stems from the dominant single-agent paradigm for physical applications, where other actors are ignored or treated as environmental noise, preventing effective coordination. Here we show that multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the essential safety scaffolding required for real-world interaction. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a high-stakes testbed, we train agents to navigate complex aerodynamic interactions and strategic maneuvering with a variable number of racers. Through league-based self-play, agents evolve sophisticated anticipatory behaviors, including proactive collision avoidance, overtaking, and handling multi-agent physical interactions, including aerodynamic downwash. Our agents outperform a champion-level human pilot in multi-player races at speeds exceeding 22 m/s, while simultaneously reducing collision rates by 50 % compared to state-of-the-art single-agent baselines. Crucially, training with diverse artificial agents enables zero-shot generalization to safer human interaction. These results suggest that the path to robust robotic co-existence lies not in isolated safety constraints, but in the rigorous demands of multi-agent interaction. Multimedia materials are available at: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl

</details>


### [193] [EvoScene-VLA: Evolving Scene Beliefs Inside the Action Decoder for Chunked Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21862)
*Chushan Zhang,Ruihan Lu,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoScene-VLA通过跨动作块传递场景状态，解决了VLA策略中动作导致几何变化但视觉更新滞后的问题，在31个RoboTwin任务和真实机器人上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略存在根本性缺陷：动作块内的后续决策依赖的几何信息（因接触、遮挡、物体运动导致）可能在下次视觉更新前已过时。空间VLA仅改进当前帧几何，时序VLA聚合历史帧，两者均无法维护跨动作块的、经动作更新的场景先验。

Method: 提出EvoScene-VLA，其核心是循环场景前缀（recurrent scene prefix），在VLM调用间传递几何感知的场景状态。每个控制调用时，VLM将当前观测信息与前一块的动作更新先验结合；动作解码器同时输出下一动作块和紧凑的场景更新。训练时使用Scene Predictor提供未来场景令牌目标，Geometric Anchor对齐冻结的深度和3D教师模型，部署时丢弃二者。

Result: 在31个RoboTwin任务上，固定评估成功率从87.2%提升至89.1%，随机化评估从86.1%提升至88.5%。在Galaxea R1-Lite真实机器人上优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 在VLA中引入跨动作块的持久化动作更新场景先验是有效的，能使每个控制调用同时利用近期动作信息和当前视觉证据，提升决策质量。

Abstract: Chunked vision-language-action (VLA) policies predict multi-step robot controls, conditioning each update on the current visual observation alone. Yet robot actions cause contact, occlusion, and object motion, and the geometry that later decisions depend on can change before the next visual update arrives. Spatial VLAs improve current-frame geometry. Temporal VLAs aggregate past frames. Neither maintains an action-updated scene prior across chunks. We argue for a persistent action-updated scene state across control calls, and introduce EvoScene-VLA. Its recurrent scene prefix carries a geometry-aware scene state across chunks. At each vision-language model (VLM) call, the VLM combines scene information from the current observation with the action-updated prior from the previous chunk; the action decoder outputs both the next action chunk and a compact scene update. This update becomes the next prior, which the VLM corrects against the new observation when the next call arrives. Each control call therefore starts from a scene prior that reflects both recent actions and fresh visual evidence. During training, \textbf{Scene Predictor} supplies future scene-token targets, and Geometric Anchor aligns scene slots with frozen depth and 3D teachers. We discard both modules at deployment. On 31 RoboTwin tasks, EvoScene-VLA raises average success from 87.2% to 89.1% in fixed evaluation and from 86.1% to 88.5% in randomized evaluation. On the Galaxea R1-Lite real robot, EvoScene-VLA outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [194] [Higher Order Reasoning for Collaborative Communicationless Mobile Robot Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21901)
*Jonathan Reasoner,Nicola Bezzo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态认知规划框架，使多机器人在无通信环境下通过高阶信念推理和行为树实现隐式协调与长时域规划，在仿真和物理实验中均优于一阶基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人系统中，通信受限或完全无通信的环境下，传统依赖持续信息交换的协调策略无法工作，亟需一种不依赖通信的隐式协调方法。

Method: 提出动态认知规划框架：机器人通过高阶信念粒子（higher-order belief particles）的形成与传播进行推理，使用贝叶斯推断更新世界信念，利用行为树（behavior tree）预测队友决策并选择动作，最后通过时间感知的MPPI控制器将推理结果集成到低层执行中。

Result: 在仿真和物理实验中，该方法一致地减少了任务完成时间，优于仅使用一阶信念的基线方法。

Conclusion: 认知逻辑可以作为通信受限环境下鲁棒协调的基础，表明高阶信念推理能够有效替代显式通信实现多机器人协同。

Abstract: In communicationless environments, multi-robot systems must operate without the constant information exchange that many coordination strategies typically assume. This paper presents a novel dynamic epistemic planning framework that enables implicit coordination and long horizon planning through higher-order reasoning among robots. With our approach, robots form and propagate higher-order belief particles, update world beliefs using Bayesian inference, and select actions via a behavior tree that anticipates teammates' likely decisions. A temporally aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller integrates this reasoning into low-level execution, allowing robots to plan intercepts and adapt trajectories under partial observability. The proposed framework is evaluated in both simulations and physical experiments, where it consistently reduces task completion time compared to a first-order baseline, demonstrating that epistemic logic can serve as a robust foundation for resilient coordination in communication-restricted domains.

</details>


### [195] [Non-Contact Vibration-Based Damage Detection of Civil Structures Using a Cost-Effective Autonomous UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21914)
*Javier Becerril,Maximiliano Vargas,Jennifer Herrera,Joanna Gutierrez,Jorge Rios,Mohsen Amjadian,Constantine Tarawneh,Jinghao Yang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低成本自主无人机（UAV）的非接触式振动结构损伤检测方法，通过视频运动跟踪提取振动信号并识别固有频率变化来检测结构损伤。实验验证了多种低成本摄像平台的有效性，其中无人机虽存在最高5.7%的误差，但能可靠检测损伤引起的频率变化，为结构健康监测提供了低成本、实用且可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接触式振动传感器在结构健康监测中存在部署不便、成本高、难以覆盖危险或难以到达区域的局限性，亟需一种非接触、低成本、灵活可扩展的检测方案。

Method: 采用自主定制的低成本无人机搭载机载摄像头，在GPS拒止环境中通过自主对齐系统进行定位；利用视觉运动跟踪从视频录製中提取振动信号；在频域中分析位移时间序列，识别固有频率偏移；与接触式加速度计和有限元模型进行对比验证。

Result: 所有低成本平台（两部智能手机、USB摄像头、自制无人机）均成功捕获了结构的基本频率及其因损伤导致的偏移；无人机平台由于自身扰动和传感限制，误差略高（最高5.7%），但能可靠检测损伤引起的频率变化；相比商用无人机系统，该平台以显著更低的成本实现了可比的检测性能。

Conclusion: 低成本自主无人机为结构健康监测提供了实用、灵活且可扩展的解决方案，特别适用于接触式传感不可行的场景。研究结果还支持未来部署多架协作无人机以进一步增强检测覆盖范围和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a non-contact approach for vibration-based structural damage detection using an autonomous and customized cost-effective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Vibration signals are extracted from video recordings through vision-based motion tracking to identify shifts in natural frequencies indicative of structural degradation. A laboratory-scale frame structure is evaluated under healthy and simulated-damage conditions. The proposed system is validated through an experimental study involving two smartphones, a USB camera, and a custom-built low-cost UAV equipped with an onboard camera and an autonomous alignment system for operation in GPS-denied environments. The displacement time is extracted and analyzed in the frequency domain and compared to reference measurements from contact accelerometers and a finite element model. Experimental results show that all platforms successfully capture the fundamental frequency and its shift due to damage. Although the UAV exhibits slightly higher errors (up to 5.7%) due to platform-induced disturbances and sensing limitations, it reliably detects damage-induced frequency changes. Compared to commercial UAV systems, the proposed platform achieves comparable inspection performance at significantly lower cost. These results demonstrate that low-cost autonomous UAVs provide a practical, flexible, and scalable solution for structural health monitoring, particularly in scenarios where contact-based sensing is impractical. The findings also support the potential for the deployment of multiple cooperative UAVs to further enhance inspection coverage and robustness.

</details>


### [196] [Quantifying Full-Body Immersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22521)
*Alihan Bakir,Ekrem Yüksel,Fabio Zuliani,Neil Chennoufi,Francesco Bruno,Jamie Paik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模块化机器人表面的全身运动交互范式，通过视听、触觉和全身沉浸三个层次，实现可扩展的虚实融合沉浸式体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实与虚拟世界的界限正在消融，现有沉浸技术多局限于视听层面，缺乏全身动态交互的深度沉浸体验，亟需一种能够实现全身运动与虚拟环境无缝融合的沉浸式平台。

Method: 提出三种沉浸层次（视听沉浸、物理沉浸、全身沉浸FBI），核心为受自然界自适应设计启发的模块化机器人表面单元，这些单元可动态分布力、形状和运动反馈，支持从个人到多用户场景的可扩展部署。

Result: 实现了可扩展、可分布的模块化平台，能够实时动态适应交互，在任意尺度下渲染沉浸环境，复现环境的物理特性，提供前所未有的全身沉浸式交互深度。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了现实与虚拟世界的鸿沟，重新定义了人与虚拟环境的共存方式，为人-环境合成的新时代奠定了基础。

Abstract: Humanity is at the forefront of yet another digital revolution, where the lines between real and virtual worlds are dissolving, reshaping how we perceive and interact with our surroundings. In this context, we introduce a transformative paradigm for immersive virtual experiences centered around whole-body kinetic interactions. Our approach redefines immersion through three distinct levels: audio-visual immersion, capturing sensory realism; physical immersion, delivering haptic feedback; and full-body immersion (FBI), where dynamic bodily interaction integrates seamlessly with virtual environments. At the core of this innovation lies a scalable, distributable platform based on modular robotic surface units inspired by the adaptive designs of nature. These units enable the rendering of immersive environments at any scale, from intimate personal experiences to expansive multi-user settings, dynamically adapting to interactions in real-time. The modular system distributes force, shape, and motion feedback throughout entire spaces, replicating the physical characteristics of the environment and enabling new depth of engagement through FBI. By combining scalability, adaptability, and dynamic physical engagement, this framework bridges the gap between real and virtual worlds. It offers an unprecedented level of immersion where users can engage their entire bodies in symbiotic interactions with the virtual space. This work not only advances immersive technology but also redefines how humans and virtual environments coexist, setting a foundation for a new era of human-environment synthesis.

</details>


### [197] [Auction-Consensus Algorithm with Learned Bidding Scheme for Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21932)
*Jose Rodriguez,Constantine Tarawneh,Sven Koenig,Wenjie Dong,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习增强的拍卖-共识框架，用基于强化学习的神经竞标策略替换CBBA的确定性贪心竞标机制，在保持去中心化执行的同时提升了多机器人任务分配的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CBBA等拍卖-共识算法依赖手工设计的贪心评分函数，导致任务分配次优，亟需一种能学习更优竞标策略的方法。

Method: 采用集中训练-去中心化执行范式，用神经竞标策略（包括NAM、LSTM、Set Transformer等架构）替换CBBA的确定性竞标机制，通过PPO算法训练，奖励由与MILP全局最优解的接近程度塑造。

Result: 在多种群规模下的实验表明，学习得到的竞标策略在保持去中心化执行的同时，优于经典CBBA的求解质量。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与经典分布式协调算法相结合，为大规模去中心化多机器人任务分配提供了一条可扩展的优化路径。

Abstract: Multi-Robot Task Allocation (MRTA) is a central challenge in decentralized multi-agent systems, where teams of robots must cooperatively assign and execute tasks under limited communication while optimizing global performance objectives. Auction-consensus algorithms, such as the Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA), provide scalable decentralized coordination with provable convergence, but rely on hand-crafted greedy scoring functions that often lead to suboptimal task allocations. This paper proposes a learning-enhanced auction-consensus framework in which CBBA's deterministic bidding mechanism is replaced by a neural bidding policy trained using reinforcement learning. Under a centralized training and decentralized execution paradigm, agents learn to compute task bids from partial local observations while retaining the standard auction and consensus phases for decentralized coordination. The learned bidding policy is trained using Proximal Policy Optimization with rewards shaped by proximity to globally optimal solutions obtained via mixed-integer linear programming. Multiple neural architectures are evaluated, including a Neural Additive Model, the Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) model, and the Set Transformer Model. Experimental results across varying swarm sizes demonstrate that learned bidding policies can improve solution quality over classical CBBA while preserving decentralized execution. The proposed approach highlights the effectiveness of integrating reinforcement learning with classical distributed coordination algorithms, offering a scalable pathway toward higher-quality decentralized multi-robot task allocation.

</details>


### [198] [Learning to Evolve: Multi-modal Interactive Fields for Robust Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21935)
*Peifeng Jiang,Hong Liu,Jin Jin,Wenshuai Wang,Xia Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态交互场（MIF），一种面向人形机器人的闭环感知-适应系统，通过置信度感知的语义3D高斯溅射、差异触发的空间记忆更新和任务驱动的几何重建，实现了在动态环境中可靠的操作导向导航，在真实动态办公室中将重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，并减少91.4%的语义内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义地图和场景图系统难以直接部署于人形机器人操作导向导航任务，因为它们通常假设稳定相机轨迹、静态环境或粗糙物体几何，无法处理运动诱导的感知畸变、环境变化和交互级别的几何安全约束。

Method: MIF构建三个耦合场：1）不确定性感知的3DGS外观场抑制步态诱导的模糊；2）空间场维护拓扑记忆；3）几何场支持操作前的交互位姿安全。引入差异检测分数区分运动诱导的假阳性变化与持久性变化，仅更新局部不一致区域，并通过特征蒸馏实现紧凑语义表示。

Result: 在宇树G1人形机器人真实动态办公室实验中，相比静态场景图记忆，MIF将非静态环境中的重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，语义内存占用减少91.4%。

Conclusion: MIF通过闭环感知-适应管道有效解决了人形机器人在动态环境中操作导航的挑战，验证了不确定性建模与差异检测机制在运动诱导畸变和环境变化下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safe manipulation-oriented navigation for humanoid robots requires scene memory that remains reliable under locomotion-induced perceptual distortion, environmental changes, and interaction-level geometric safety constraints. Existing semantic mapping and scene-graph systems are difficult to deploy directly in this setting because they often assume stable camera trajectories, static environments, or coarse object geometry. We introduce the Multi-modal Interactive Field (MIF), a humanoid-oriented system that integrates confidence-aware semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting, discrepancy-triggered spatial memory updates, and task-driven geometric reconstruction within a closed-loop perception-adaptation pipeline. MIF couples three fields: an uncertainty-aware 3DGS Appearance Field that suppresses gait-induced blur, a Spatial Field that maintains topological memory, and a Geometry Field that supports Interaction Pose Safety (IPS) before manipulation. A discrepancy detection score is introduced to separate locomotion-induced false-positive changes from persistent changes and updates only locally inconsistent regions. On a Unitree-G1 humanoid in a real dynamic office, MIF improves relocation success in non-static environments from 12% to 94% compared with static scene-graph memory, while reducing semantic memory footprint by 91.4% through feature distillation for practical online operation. Project page and code: https://ziya-jiang.github.io/MIF-homepage/

</details>


### [199] [A Visitation Grid for Complete Coverage Foraging in Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21947)
*Qi Arturo Gonzalez,Yifeng Gao,Li Zhang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于网格的随机觅食策略，通过维护全局访问密度估计并引导机器人优先探索未充分访问区域，在最后阶段显著提升收集效率，比经典CPFA算法降低33%总收集时间，最后阶段效率提升超48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群机器人觅食算法在最后阶段（仅剩少量分散资源时）消耗大量任务时间，而多数算法仅关注有限时间内收集大部分资源，缺乏对末期效率的优化。

Method: 将未知搜索区域划分为网格地图，由轻量级中央服务器维护全局访问计数；每个觅食行程中，机器人从局部3×3邻域内按访问计数概率选择下一搜索区域，偏向低访问区域同时保持随机性。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提策略相比CPFA总收集时间降低33%，最后阶段收集效率提升超48%，且具有鲁棒性、灵活性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该策略可作为群机器人随机觅食方法的通用增强模块，在有限机载资源下实现近乎完整和完整的资源收集。

Abstract: The complete collection of sparse resources in large, unknown environments remains a challenging problem for autonomous robot swarms. Previous studies have shown that a substantial portion of total mission time is consumed during the final stage of collection, where only a small fraction of randomly scattered resources remain. Consequently, many existing swarm foraging algorithms (search and collection) focus on collecting most resources within a limited time window, rather than improving end-stage efficiency for collecting all resources. We propose a grid-based stochastic foraging strategy that explicitly reduces redundant visits and accelerates late-stage collection. The unknown search area is partitioned into a grid map, which is maintained by a lightweight central server. To maintain scalability, both robots and the server operate within limited memory and computational constraints. The server updates the grid-level visitation counts based on robot-reported locations, producing a global estimate of the exploration density. For each new foraging trip, a robot selects its next search area from a local 3 X 3 neighborhood of grids probabilistically with the lowest visitation count, thus biasing exploration toward under-visited regions while maintaining stochasticity. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that the proposed strategy consistently outperforms the canonical centrally placed baseline foraging algorithm (CPFA). Compared to CPFA, the proposed method reduces the total collection time by up to 33% and improves collection efficiency by more than 48% during the final stage of the mission. These results indicate that the proposed strategy is robust, flexible, and scalable for near-complete and complete resource collection in robot swarms and can serve as a general enhancement for stochastic swarm foraging methods under limited onboard resources.

</details>


### [200] [TacO: Benchmarking Tactile Sensors for Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21976)
*Anya Zorin,Zilin Si,Myungsun Park,Junsung Park,Alexiy Buynitsky,Sachin Bhadang,Taejun Park,Sohee John Yoon,Yong-Lae Park,Oliver Kroemer,Zeynep Temel,Michael T. Tolley,Sha Yi,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对四种触觉传感器模态（视觉、声学、磁学、电阻式）在三种机器人操作任务（拾放未知质量物体、物体重定向、插销插入）中进行了系统评估，发现触觉传感器的有效性高度依赖于传感器模态、材料属性和具体任务，而非普遍适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然触觉感知被广泛认为能改善机器人操作，但缺乏关于哪种触觉传感器最适合哪种操作任务的实证指导。

Method: 针对四种不同模态的触觉传感器（视觉、声学、磁学、电阻式），分别在三个操作任务（拾放未知质量物体、物体重定向、插销插入）上训练独立的操作策略，并分析传感器属性（空间分辨率、剪切感知、触觉表征）和材料摩擦对任务性能的影响。

Result: 触觉信息的有用性强烈依赖于传感器模态、材料属性和具体操作任务，而非普遍有益。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于操作策略性能的触觉传感器选择和评估框架，所有传感器、代码、数据和硬件设置将公开。

Abstract: Vision-based learning from demonstrations has achieved remarkable success in enabling robots to perform manipulation tasks and high-level semantic reasoning, yet it remains insufficient for complex, contact-rich manipulation. While there is broad agreement that tactile sensing improves manipulation, there is no empirical guidance on which tactile sensors are best suited for which manipulation tasks. In this paper, we provide a systematic, task-driven evaluation of tactile sensors for robot manipulation and propose a framework for selecting and evaluating sensors based on manipulation policy performance. Separate manipulation policies are trained for tactile sensors of four distinct modalities: visual, acoustic, magnetic, and resistive, across three tasks: pick-and-place with unknown mass, object reorientation, and plug insertion. For each task, an analysis of how sensor properties such as spatial resolution, shear sensing, and tactile representation, and the inherent material friction affect task performances is done. Rather than tactile sensing being universally beneficial in the same way, our results show that the usefulness of tactile information depends strongly on sensor modality, material properties, and the specific manipulation tasks. All of the tactile sensors, code, data, and hardware setup will be publicly available on the project website.

</details>


### [201] [Industrial Dual-Arm Box Handling via Online Inertial Estimation and Convex Wrench Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22021)
*Kenzhi Iskandar Wong,Lin Yang,Qian Ying Lee,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对未知惯性参数的工业箱体操作问题，提出了一种摩擦感知的双臂抓取框架，通过在线估计质量和质心、基于二阶锥规划求解摩擦可行的接触力与扭转力矩，并在离线轨迹优化中减少非必要的环境接触，实验验证了稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人操作中，箱体等物体的质量和质心通常未知，导致力-力矩平衡难以维持，易发生滑移、掉落、姿态偏离或过度挤压等问题，现有方法缺少统一的摩擦可行约束处理。

Method: （1）在线估计：基于实测接触力/力矩在线估计物体质量和质心；（2）SOCP优化：在椭球摩擦极限面约束下，通过二阶锥规划求解摩擦可行的接触力和扭转力矩；（3）硬约束处理：将摩擦可行性作为硬约束，在可行域内最小化接触力，避免独立调节滑移与挤压目标；（4）离线轨迹修正：减少几何约束下的非期望环境接触。

Result: 在真实双臂机器人系统上，针对不同质心配置的物体，该方法能够稳定抓取未知惯性参数的箱体，维持摩擦接触稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过在线惯性参数估计与摩擦可行约束优化，实现了对未知惯性参数物体的稳定抓取，无需单独调参处理滑移与过度挤压问题，兼具鲁棒性与实用性。

Abstract: Industrial robotic object handling often involves boxes and packages whose mass and center of mass are not known in advance. These uncertainties affect the force--moment balance required for stable lifting, and improper regulation of contact wrenches can lead to slip, object drop, orientation deviation, or excessive squeezing. This paper presents a friction-aware dual-arm box-handling framework for objects with unknown inertial properties. The proposed approach estimates the object mass and center of mass online from measured contact wrenches, and computes friction-feasible contact forces and torsional moments through a second-order cone program (SOCP) under ellipsoidal friction-limit-surface constraints. An offline trajectory refinement stage is also included to reduce undesired object--environment contact when geometric constraints are present. By enforcing friction feasibility as a hard constraint and minimizing contact effort within the feasible region, the framework achieves stable lifting without treating slip avoidance and excessive squeezing as separately tuned objectives. Experiments on a real dual-arm robotic system under different center-of-mass configurations demonstrate that the method lifts objects with unknown inertial properties while maintaining stable frictional contact.

</details>


### [202] [CoRMA: Contrastive RMA for Contact-Rich Meta-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22082)
*Wentian Wang,Chutong Wen,Hongxu Ma,Wuhao Wang,Zhexiong Xue,Abdul Haseeb Nizamani,Dandi Zhou,Xinhai Sun,Jianqiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRMA提出了一种基于对比学习的上下文元适应框架，用6维语义接触上下文替代原始RMA中的模拟器参数适应，通过因果Transformer在线推断接触状态，在力控装配任务中实现了无需演示、特权信息或梯度更新的在线适应，在仿真和真实机器人上优于FORGE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMA在力控装配任务中表现不佳，因为模拟器参数适应方式难以应对力主导场景中的接触复杂性；同时FORGE等基线在仿真中成功率高但迁移到真实硬件时性能显著下降。

Method: 1. 用6维模拟器内语义接触上下文（接触起始、横向啮合、引导过渡、接触方向、卡滞）替代原始模拟器参数适应；2. 部署时可用的因果Transformer适配器通过语义回归和力区间对比学习目标，从力、本体感知和动作历史中在线推断该上下文；3. 部署时移除oracle上下文，替换为推断的上下文。

Result: 在PegInsert、GearMesh、NutThread三个装配任务及真实Marvin机械臂上，CoRMA相比FORGE基线在受控目标位姿噪声下保持了更高的真实成功率，而FORGE从仿真到硬件时性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 语义接触推断可作为相关装配任务族内可复用的适应接口，但更广泛的未见任务泛化及Real2Sim校准是未来工作。

Abstract: We present CoRMA(Contrastive Robotic Motor Adaptation), a context-based meta-adaptation framework that modifies RMA for force-dominant assembly. CoRMA replaces raw simulator-parameter adaptation with a compact 6D simulator-only semantic contact context describing contact onset, lateral engagement, guided transition, contact direction, and jamming. A deployable causal Transformer adapter infers this context online from force, proprioceptive, and action histories using semantic regression and a force-regime contrastive objective. At deployment, oracle context is removed and replaced by the inferred context, enabling within-episode adaptation without demonstrations, privileged inputs, or gradient updates. We evaluate CoRMA on PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread in Isaac Lab / Isaac Sim~5.0 and on a real Marvin arm. Compared with FORGE baselines that achieve high simulation success but degrade substantially on hardware, CoRMA retains higher verified real success under controlled target-pose noise. These results support semantic contact inference as a reusable adaptation interface within a related assembly task family, while broader unseen-task generalization and Real2Sim calibration remain future work.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Pixels: Learning Invariant Rewards for Real-World Robotics From a Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22123)
*Tengye Xu,Yangting Sun,Ziju Shen,Guanqi Chen,Zhen Fu,Chen yizhou,Hua Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从少量演示中学习不变符号奖励函数的框架，通过发现行为不变量替代视觉特征拟合，实现奖励函数在位置、视角和物体变化下的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉奖励模型容易记忆特定像素分布，无法泛化到实验室环境之外的开放世界操作任务中不同物体实例、位置和视角的变化。

Method: 框架包含两个耦合组件：1) 结构奖励公式，编码任务级策略和物理约束，保持最优策略不变性；2) 混合符号-数值过程，从少量演示中蒸馏行为不变量，无需在线交互。

Result: 在8个Meta-World任务和3个Franka操作任务上，方法在过程对齐和策略 rollout 排序能力上优于基线，加速下游策略学习。三个真实世界OOD实验显示奖励在位置、视角和物体变化下实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 通过学习行为不变量而非视觉特征，单奖励表示可复用于多种任务变体，显著提升开放世界操作任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Designing reward functions that generalize beyond controlled laboratory settings remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning for robotics. In open-world manipulation problems, a single task can appear in numerous variants through different object instances, positions, and camera viewpoints. Recent vision-based reward models tend to memorize specific pixel distributions and fail to generalize beyond their training conditions. To address this, we propose a framework that learns invariant symbolic reward functions from as few as five demonstrations. The insight is to shift from visual feature-fitting to the discovery of behavioral invariants: task-level properties that remain constant across diverse visual instantiations. The framework has two coupled components: a structural reward formulation that encodes task-level strategies and physical constraints while preserving optimal policy invariance, and a hybrid symbolic-numerical procedure that distills these invariants from demonstrations without online interaction. Experiments on eight Meta-World tasks and three Franka manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method achieves stronger process alignment and policy rollout ranking abilities compared to baselines, accelerating downstream policy learning. Three real-world out-of-distribution experiments further show that the same learned reward generalizes zero-shot to position, viewpoint, and object variations, enabling a single reward representation to be reused across diverse task variants in practice.

</details>


### [204] [Action with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22183)
*Weilong Guo,Yuchen Wang,Renping Zhou,Yunfeng Zhang,Rui Fang,Yue Meng,Wenda Xu,Yuan He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AVP是一种端到端架构，通过让VLM推断下一阶段目标并发出视觉基元标记来条件化流匹配动作专家，将认知感知与动作控制解耦，相比pi_0.5在通用拾取任务上提升了27.61%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型将语言指令理解、空间场景感知和运动控制耦合在单一学习目标中，导致动作专家需要隐式重新学习预训练VLM中已具备的认知和感知能力，限制了学习效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出AVP架构，VLM从视觉和语言输入中推断下一阶段目标并生成视觉基元标记（visual primitive tokens），这些标记作为条件输入流匹配（flow-matching）动作专家，动作专家通过末端执行器运动学监督进行训练，实现认知推理与运动生成的解耦。

Result: 在通用拾取任务的真实机器人实验中，AVP相比pi_0.5提升了27.61%的成功率，并且在数据效率、空间组合泛化和物体级迁移方面均表现出一致的优势。

Conclusion: 通过视觉基元作为中间接口解耦认知与动作，能够有效提升VLA模型的学习效率和泛化能力，验证了该设计在机器人操控任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation. A common design in current architectures maps language instructions and visual observations to actions in a single forward pass. While conceptually simple, this formulation entangles instruction comprehension, spatial scene understanding, and motor control within a single learning objective. As a result, the action expert must implicitly relearn cognitive and perceptual capabilities already present in the pretrained VLM, which can limit both learning efficiency and generalization. We introduce AVP (Action with Visual Primitives), an end-to-end architecture that implements this visual-primitive-centric interface: the VLM infers the next-stage target and emits visual-primitive tokens that condition a flow-matching action expert, with supervision derived from end-effector kinematics. Real-robot experiments on general pick-and-place tasks show that AVP improves the success rate by 27.61% over pi_0.5 and outperforms other recent methods, with consistent gains in data efficiency, spatial-compositional generalization, and object-level transfer.

</details>


### [205] [Learning A Unified Risk Map for Autonomous Driving in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22189)
*Jie Jia,Yaofeng Su,Zeyu Bao,Yun Hong,Bingzhao Gao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向部分可观测环境的统一风险地图建模与学习框架，通过集成交通流风险与碰撞风险的时空建模，并引入基于扩散的对抗场景生成，实现了遮挡条件下的风险感知规划。在Waymo数据集上，最小碰撞时间提升0.78倍，平均碰撞时间提升1.67倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么基于可达状态过高估计风险，要么在高遮挡不确定性下难以预测准确轨迹，缺乏统一的遮挡感知风险建模与学习框架。

Method: (1) 通过时空建模集成交通流风险与碰撞风险，实现遮挡诱导风险的细粒度评估；(2) 引入基于扩散模型的场景生成框架，生成真实且对抗性的遮挡交互场景；(3) 将统一风险地图的建模与学习集成到部分可观测条件下的风险感知规划框架中。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，最小碰撞时间提升0.78倍，平均碰撞时间提升1.67倍，显著超越现有最优遮挡感知基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为部分可观测环境下的风险感知规划提供了一种全面且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Occlusion-aware prediction remains a critical challenge in autonomous driving due to the inherent uncertainty of unobserved regions. Existing approaches either overestimate risk based on reachable states or struggle to predict accurate trajectories under high occlusion uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a unified risk map modeling and learning framework for partially observable environments. Our method integrates traffic flow risk and collision risk through spatiotemporal modeling, enabling fine-grained assessment of occlusion-induced hazards. To address the scarcity of scenarios involving occluded interactions, we introduce a diffusion-based scenario generation framework that produces realistic yet adversarial scenarios. We integrate the modeling and learning of a unified risk map into a framework that supports risk-aware planning under partial observability. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art occlusion-aware baseline, improving minimum time-to-collision by 0.78 times and average time-to-collision by 1.67 times. The proposed framework offers a comprehensive and practical solution for risk-aware planning in partially observable environments.

</details>


### [206] [Imagine2Real: Towards Zero-shot Humanoid-Object Interaction via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22272)
*Jiahe Chen,ZiRui Wang,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Xiaojie Niu,Weishuai Zeng,Tianfan Xue,Xiaowei Zhou,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Imagine2Real是一个零样本人形机器人-物体交互框架，通过将运动表示为统一4D点轨迹并仅追踪稀疏关键点，绕过了传统方法中的几何先验依赖和重定向复杂性，利用行为基础模型潜空间和渐进训练策略实现零样本物理部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身人形机器人-物体交互方法受限于高质量3D数据的稀缺性，视频生成先验虽具潜力但存在两个瓶颈：一是依赖几何先验（如显式CAD模型）导致的表示不对齐问题；二是密集变形和形态不匹配引发的重定向复杂性。

Method: 1) 将机器人和物体的运动统一表示为4D点轨迹，解决表示不对齐；2) 提出Keypoints Tracker仅追踪稀疏关键点（基座、手部和物体），完全绕过重定向过程；3) 利用行为基础模型的潜空间作为追踪器的搜索域以维持自然步态；4) 采用渐进训练策略，仅通过简单追踪奖励学习鲁棒行为。

Result: 实现零样本物理部署，可在动作捕捉系统内直接运行，无需针对特定物体或场景进行微调。

Conclusion: Imagine2Real通过统一4D轨迹表示和稀疏关键点追踪，有效解决了人形机器人-物体交互中的表示不对齐和重定向复杂性问题，为零样本物理交互提供了一种灵活、无几何依赖的解决方案。

Abstract: Whole-body Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D data. While video generative priors offer a promising alternative, existing methods suffer from \textit{Representation Misalignment} due to their reliance on geometric priors (e.g., explicit CAD models), and \textit{Retargeting Complexity} arising from intensive morphing and morphological mismatch. We propose Imagine2Real, a zero-shot HOI framework for flexible, geometry-free interaction. To resolve misalignment, we formulate robot and object motions as unified 4D point trajectories. To overcome retargeting complexity, our Keypoints Tracker tracks only sparse critical points (base, hands, and object), entirely bypassing the error-amplifying retargeting process. To maintain natural gaits despite these sparse signals, we utilize the latent space of a Behavior Foundation Model (BFM) as the tracker's search domain. Using a progressive training strategy, Imagine2Real learns robust behaviors with simple tracking rewards, enabling zero-shot physical deployment within a motion capture(mocap) system.

</details>


### [207] [Spatial Memory for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22283)
*Pengteng Li,Weiyu Guo,He Zhang,Tiefu Cai,Xiao He,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SOMA，一种为视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）赋予空间记忆的框架，使其能够在目标物体超出相机视野时仍能完成操作任务。SOMA通过多视角扫描构建持久空间记忆，并包含动态记忆更新与上下文检索机制，在多项真实世界任务中显著提升了成功率与操作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型默认任务相关物体始终可见，当目标物体超出相机视野时，模型表现出脆弱和被动的行为，缺乏对不可见目标的推理能力。

Method: SOMA包含三个核心组件：1) 空间记忆构建（Spatial Memory Construction）：通过可移动头部摄像头进行多视角扫描，聚合角度的观测为统一的空间-语义表示；2) 动态记忆优化（Dynamic Memory Refinement）：随时间维护全局一致性；3) 上下文记忆检索（Contextual Memory Retrieval）：在操作过程中激活与指令相关的空间线索。

Result: 在五项具有挑战性的真实世界超视野操作任务中，SOMA不仅提高了任务成功率，还展现出更快的目标定位、更少的视角搜索以及近乎一次性的抓取能力。在RoboCasa GR1和SimplerEnv上的额外实验进一步验证了其在常规完全可观测设置下的有效性。

Conclusion: SOMA通过引入持久空间记忆，有效解决了VLA模型在目标物体超出视野时的推理与操作难题，显著提升了模型的鲁棒性和操作效率。

Abstract: We introduce SOMA, the Spatial Memory framework for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Most existing VLAs implicitly assume that task-relevant objects are always visible, leading to brittle and reactive behaviors when targets fall outside the camera's field of view. SOMA addresses this limitation by equipping VLAs with a persistent spatial memory constructed from multi-view observations acquired via a movable head camera, enabling reasoning beyond the current visual frustum. The framework consists of three components: Spatial Memory Construction, which aggregates angular-wise observations into a unified spatial-semantic representation through scanning; Dynamic Memory Refinement, which maintains global consistency over time; and Contextual Memory Retrieval, which activates instruction-relevant spatial cues during manipulation. We evaluate SOMA on five challenging real-world out-of-vision manipulation tasks, including multi-step and dual-arm scenarios where target objects are initially invisible. Experimental results show that SOMA not only improves task success rates, but also induces qualitatively different manipulation behaviors, with faster target localization, reduced viewpoint search, and near one-shot grasping under partial observability. Additional experiments on RoboCasa GR1 and SimplerEnv further validate the effectiveness of SOMA's memory design under conventional fully observable settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [208] [How can reasoning capability empower the AI copilot robot in endoscopic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22322)
*Guankun Wang,Long Bai,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了推理能力在基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的AI副手手术机器人中的应用潜力，提出推理驱动的自主性可将机器人从被动执行者转变为认知协作者，提升内镜手术的精确性、安全性和可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理能力在通用领域的复杂逻辑推理和机器人决策中已取得显著进展，但在内镜手术领域的AI副手机器人（特别是基于VLA模型）中尚未得到探索。手术环境中的不确定性及外科医生的认知负担亟待通过有效的多模态推理来缓解。

Method: 提出将推理能力集成到基于VLA模型的AI副手机器人中，使其能够整合多模态线索、解读手术意图并推断隐藏的组织动力学。

Result: 推理驱动的自主性可使AI副手机器人从被动执行者转变为认知协作者，提升临床实践中的精确性、安全性和可持续性。

Conclusion: 推理能力是提升内镜手术AI副手机器人智能化水平的关键，有望显著改善手术效果和外科医生的工作体验。

Abstract: Reasoning capability has significantly advanced complex logical inference and robotic decision-making in general domains. However, its potential in the Artificial Intelligence (AI) copilot robot-particularly implemented based on the Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model-remains unexplored in endoscopic surgery. Effective reasoning should enable AI copilot robots to integrate multimodal cues, interpret surgical intent, and infer hidden tissue dynamics, thereby alleviating intraoperative uncertainty and cognitive burden on surgeons. Properly implemented, reasoning-driven autonomy can transform AI copilot robots from reactive executors into cognitive collaborators, enhancing precision, safety, and sustainability in clinical practice.

</details>


### [209] [Real-Time Auto-Optimization in Unknown Environments via Structure-Exploiting Dual Control for Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22431)
*Shiying Dong,Haoyang Yang,Qiwei Liu,Wen-Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速数值双控探索与利用（DCEE）方法，利用奖励函数固有的凸-非线性结构，通过仅线性化非线性残差映射并保留凸外层损失，将子问题转化为结构化凸形式，在车辆巡航自优化问题中实现了约一个数量级的加速，最大计算时间仅83微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知环境中自优化问题的计算负担瓶颈，现有DCEE方法未充分利用其数值结构，导致计算效率低下，难以满足在线实时控制需求。

Method: 揭示了DCEE奖励函数具有凸-非线性结构（凸外层损失+非线性残差映射），提出仅线性化非线性残差映射而保留凸外层损失的广义高斯-牛顿方法，使每个子问题变为可可靠求解的结构化凸形式，Hessian近似仅依赖一阶导数且半正定。

Result: 在车辆巡航自优化问题上，相比现有方法，控制性能提升，计算加速约一个数量级，在典型车载嵌入式CPU上最大计算时间仅83微秒（微秒级）。

Conclusion: 所提结构利用方法有效解决了DCEE的计算效率瓶颈，实现了微秒级在线求解，适用于实时控制场景。

Abstract: This paper develops a fast numerical dual control for exploration and exploitation (DCEE) method to address auto-optimization problems in unknown environments. In auto-optimization problems, the optimal operating condition is unknown a priori and may vary with the environment. As in classical dual control techniques, computational burden remains a major concern in DCEE for active learning. Existing DCEE methods provide a principled exploration-exploitation objective, but mainly realized through standard optimization packages or explicit gradient-type update laws, where the numerical structure of the DCEE has not been fully exploited. This paper shows that the reward function in DCEE has an inherent convex-over-nonlinear structure, where the exploitation and exploration terms form a unified nonlinear residual map equipped with a convex outer loss. Benefiting from this structure, a structure-exploiting numerical method is developed by linearizing only the nonlinear residual map while preserving the convex outer loss. Thus, each subproblem is transformed into a structured convex form that can be solved reliably. The resulting generalized Gauss-Newton Hessian approximation is positive semidefinite and depends only on first-order derivatives, thereby supporting fast online computation. The proposed method is evaluated on a vehicle cruising auto-optimization problem and compared with existing methods. Simulation and hardware-in-the-loop experimental results show that the proposed method improves control performance and achieves a speedup of approximately one order of magnitude, with a microsecond-level maximum computation time of only 83 μs on a typical vehicle embedded CPU.

</details>


### [210] [Terminal Constraint Model Predictive Control for Image-Based Visual Servoing of UAVs with Kalman Filter-Based Moment Loss Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22443)
*X. Wang,Y. Cao,W. L. W. Leong,Y. R. Tan,S. Huang,S. H. R. Teo,C. Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对IBVS中近目标的稳定性丧失和视觉特征丢失导致控制失效问题，提出了融合终端约束模型预测控制（TC-MPC）与卡尔曼滤波状态预测的UAV视觉伺服框架，通过终端约束和代价保证递归可行性与闭环稳定性，利用KF预测图像矩克服视觉退化，实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBVS控制器在UAV视觉伺服中存在两个关键缺陷：一是近目标时输入/状态约束导致闭环稳定性丧失，二是剧烈运动下基于矩的视觉特征间歇性丢失导致控制失败。

Method: 提出终端约束模型预测控制（TC-MPC）框架，将终端状态约束和终端代价显式融入IBVS误差动力学中；同时集成基于卡尔曼滤波的状态预测机制，在视觉短暂退化时预测图像矩的时域演化。

Result: 通过实时UAV视觉伺服实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TC-MPC结合卡尔曼滤波能够保证递归可行性、改善收敛行为、在约束条件下维持闭环稳定性，并在视觉特征部分不可用时保持控制连续性。

Abstract: Image-Based Visual Servoing (IBVS) provides an efficient vision-guided control paradigm for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) by directly regulating image-space errors. However, conventional IBVS controllers are vulnerable to two critical issues: loss of closed-loop stability near the target due to input and state constraints, and control failure caused by intermittent loss of moment-based visual features under aggressive motion. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a terminal-constraint model predictive control (TC-MPC) framework for IBVS, integrated with a Kalman filter (KF)-based state-prediction mechanism. The TC-MPC explicitly incorporates terminal-state constraints and a terminal cost into the IBVS error dynamics, ensuring recursive feasibility, improved convergence behavior, and closed-loop stability under control and state constraints. In parallel, the Kalman filter predicts the temporal evolution of image moments during short-term visual degradation, enabling the controller to preserve control continuity when moment measurements are partially unavailable. The proposed approach is validated through real-time UAV visual servoing experiments.

</details>


### [211] [Steins;Gate Drive: Semantic Safety Arbitration over Structured Futures for Latency-Decoupled LLM Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22456)
*Anjie Qiu,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SteinsGateDrive 提出了一种延迟解耦的规划-运行时架构，通过世界线隐喻让 LLM 提前选择反事实驾驶未来，并在运行时复用安全合约内的预测，有效消除了 LLM 推理延迟对车辆控制的影响。在高速场景下，GPT-5.4 mini 的延迟从 +3.07 秒（1秒视界）降至 -0.01 秒（4秒视界），同时保持无碰撞安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管 LLM 驱动的智能体能提供有用的语义判断，但其推理延迟超过了逐步行车控制的时间窗口；已有的学习型世界模型虽能预测未来，但通常将未来生成和动作选择捆绑在大型耦合循环中，导致延迟问题无法分离解决。

Method: 提出 SteinsGateDrive 架构，核心是 LLM 在最终控制时刻之前选择反事实驾驶未来（世界线），运行时仅在安全合约有效期间复用该预测。生成器构建三种世界线角色：阿尔法（名义自我条件未来）、贝塔（附近车辆的交互反事实）、伽马（危险应激未来如刹车、切入或堵塞通道）。选中的分支变为带视界、有效性/中止条件、回退和权限的类型化 StrategicForecast。运行时通过原子谓词检查保障安全。

Result: 在相同受试者、匹配种子的正常高速公路协议下（10个种子，20步），GPT-5.4 mini 将有效延迟从 1秒视界下的 +3.07 秒降至 4秒视界下的 -0.01 秒，同时保持无碰撞安全边界。安全贡献来自原子谓词运行时检查，而非漂移分数（漂移分数仅作为刷新频率旋钮）。

Conclusion: SteinsGateDrive 通过将 LLM 的语义推理与实时控制解耦，在保证安全的前提下几乎完全消除了 LLM 推理延迟对驾驶控制的影响，为云托管 LLM 智能体在实时控制场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted LLM driver agents provide useful semantic judgments, but their inference latency exceeds stepwise vehicle-control windows. Learned world models predict futures, but they usually keep future generation and action selection inside large coupled loops. We present SteinsGateDrive, a latency-decoupled planner-runtime architecture in which the worldline metaphor from the eponymous story names one plausible consequence of an intervention: the LLM selects counterfactual driving futures before the final control instant, and a runtime reuses the selected forecast only while safety contracts remain valid. The generator builds three world-line roles: alpha nominal ego-conditioned futures, beta interaction counterfactuals around nearby vehicles, and gamma hazard-stress futures such as braking, cut-ins, or blocked corridors. The selected branch becomes a typed StrategicForecast with horizon, validity/abort conditions, fallback, and authority. On a within-subject, matched-seed normal-highway protocol with 10 seeds and 20 steps, GPT-5.4 mini reduces effective lag from +3.07 s at 1-second horizon to -0.01 s at 4-second horizon while preserving the measured no-collision safety boundary. The architecture's safety contribution comes from the atom-predicate runtime check, not from the drift score, which functions as a refresh-frequency knob.

</details>


### [212] [SE3Kit: A Lightweight Python Library for Specialized Geometric Primitives in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22633)
*Daniyal Maroufi,Omid Rezayof,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SE3Kit是一个轻量级、纯Python实现的李群运算库，专注于SE(3)和SO(3)的严格数学操作，填补了现有库在轻量级与数学严谨性之间的空白，适用于嵌入式部署、快速原型设计和教育场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python机器人学领域缺乏既轻量又数学严谨的刚体变换库，现有方案要么依赖繁重框架（如SpatialMath、PyPose），要么缺乏机器人专用功能（如SciPy）。

Method: 采用纯Python和NumPy实现SE(3)和SO(3)的李群运算，无深度学习或可视化软件的额外开销，提供严格数学实现。

Result: SE3Kit实现了在轻量级与数学严谨性之间的平衡，无需繁重依赖即可完成专用李群运算。

Conclusion: SE3Kit填补了Python机器人学中轻量级且数学严谨的刚体变换库的空白，适合嵌入式、快速原型和教育场景。

Abstract: The Python robotics ecosystem faces a challenge: while many libraries exist for rigid body transformations, few are both lightweight and mathematically strict. This paper introduces SE3Kit, a lightweight Python library efficient operations on the Special Euclidean Group SE(3) and the Special Orthogonal Group SO(3). Unlike established frameworks that require heavy dependencies (e.g., SpatialMath, PyPose) or general tools that lack robotics-specific features (e.g., SciPy), SE3Kit targets the gap between these extremes. It is designed for embedded deployment, rapid prototyping, and education while providing rigorous mathematical implementation. It provides a pure-Python, NumPy-only implementation of Lie Group operations, without the overhead of deep learning or other visualization software.

</details>


### [213] [Symmetries Here and There, Combined Everywhere: Cross-space Symmetry Compositions in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22639)
*Loizos Hadjiloizou,Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出跨空间对称性组合框架，利用前向运动学的微分几何结构，在配置空间和任务空间之间传递对称性，实现多种对称性的联合等变性，并在双机机器人上验证了其泛化性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常孤立处理机器人问题中同时存在的多种对称性，未能充分利用其组合潜力，导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出跨空间对称性组合框架，利用前向运动学的微分几何结构，将对称性从配置空间降维传递到任务空间，以及从任务空间提升到配置空间，在统一表示空间内实现多种对称性的组合等变策略学习。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的双机机器人实验中，联合利用多种对称性相比孤立处理方法显著提升了策略的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 跨空间对称性组合框架能有效融合机器人结构和任务中的多重对称性，提高策略的泛化能力，具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: Robots exhibit a rich variety of symmetries arising from their mechanical structure and the properties of their tasks. Although many robotics problems exhibit several symmetries simultaneously, existing approaches typically treat them in isolation, failing to exploit their combined potential. This paper introduces cross-space symmetry compositions, a framework for learning robot policies that are jointly equivariant to multiple symmetries across configuration and task spaces. Leveraging the differential-geometric structure of the forward kinematics map, we both descend symmetries from configuration to task space and lift symmetries from task to configuration space, enabling their composition within a unified representation space. We validate our framework on simulated and real-world experiments on a dual-arm robot, demonstrating that jointly leveraging multiple symmetries yields improved generalization.

</details>


### [214] [GesVLA: Gesture-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model Embedded Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22812)
*Wenxuan Guo,Ziyuan Li,Meng Zhang,Yichen Liu,Yimeng Dong,Chuxi Xu,Yunfei Wei,Ze Chen,Erjin Zhou,Jianjiang Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GesVLA通过将手势作为并行指令模态引入VLA模型，采用双VLM架构和渲染手模型的数据生成流水线，显著提升了复杂场景下的目标定位精度和人机交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖文本指令，在包含多个相似物体的复杂场景中难以解决空间歧义问题，限制了其在通用机器人操作中的实用性。

Method: (1) 将手势特征编码到潜在空间，同时参与高层推理和低层动作生成；(2) 采用双VLM架构实现手势表示与动作策略的紧耦合；(3) 构建可扩展的手势数据生成流水线，通过将手模型渲染到真实场景图像上减少仿真到现实的视觉差异；(4) 采用两阶段训练策略分别训练手势感知和动作预测能力。

Result: 在多种真实机器人任务（包括积木操作验证实验及更实用的产品和农产品选择任务）上，引入手势一致性地提升了目标定位准确性和人机交互效率，尤其在复杂杂乱环境中效果显著。

Conclusion: 手势作为并行指令模态能够有效解决VLA模型在复杂场景中的空间歧义问题，提升机器人操作的鲁棒性和交互效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation by unifying perception and action. However, existing VLA systems primarily rely on textual instructions and struggle to resolve spatial ambiguity in complex scenes with multiple similar objects. To address this limitation, we introduce gesture as a parallel instruction modality and propose a Gesture-aware Vision-Language-Action model (GesVLA). Our approach encodes gesture features directly into the latent space, enabling them to participate in both high-level reasoning and low-level action generation, and adopts a dual-VLM architecture to achieve tight coupling between gesture representations and action policies. At the data level, we construct a scalable gesture data generation pipeline by rendering hand models onto real-world scene images. This reduces the sim-to-real visual gap while producing rich data with diverse motion patterns and corresponding pointing annotations. In addition, we employ a two-stage training strategy to equip the model with both gesture perception and action prediction capabilities. We evaluate our approach on multiple real-world robotic tasks, including a controlled block manipulation task for validation and more practical scenarios such as product and produce selection. Experimental results show that incorporating gesture consistently improves target grounding accuracy and human-robot interaction efficiency, especially in complex and cluttered environments. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/.

</details>


### [215] [AwareVLN: Reasoning with Self-awareness for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22816)
*Wenxuan Guo,Xiuwei Xu,Yichen Liu,Xiangyu Li,Hang Yin,Huangxing Chen,Wenzhao Zheng,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AwareVLN通过引入结构化的自感知推理模块和自动数据引擎，使VLN模型在端到端框架下理解智能体状态和任务进度，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法要么依赖VLM进行端到端动作预测但缺乏可解释的智能体-指令-场景关系理解，要么构建场景地图进行启发式规划但依赖额外3D传感器且阻碍大规模视觉-语言预训练。为弥合这一差距，提出一种兼具端到端可训练性和自感知推理能力的框架。

Method: (1) 结构推理模块：赋予模型空间和任务导向的自我感知能力；(2) 自动数据引擎：通过进度划分实现高效训练。整个框架采用完全端到端、数据驱动的方式。

Result: 在Habitat模拟器的多个数据集上，AwareVLN显著优于此前最先进的视觉-语言导航方法。

Conclusion: 通过在端到端框架中引入自感知推理，有效桥接了可解释的结构化理解与大规模预训练之间的鸿沟，为VLN任务提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to ground language instructions to its own movement within a visual environment. While state-of-the-art methods leverage the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for end-to-end action prediction, they often lack an explicit and explainable understanding of the relationships between the agent, the instruction, and the scene. Conversely, explicitly building a scene map for heuristic planning is intuitively appealing but relies on additional 3D sensors and hinders large-scale vision-language pre-training. To bridge this gap, we propose AwareVLN, a novel framework that equips the navigation model with a self-aware reasoning mechanism, enabling it to understand the agent's state and task progress in a fully end-to-end and data-driven manner. Our approach features two key innovations: (1) a structural reasoning module that fosters spatial and task-oriented self-awareness, and (2) an automatic data engine with progress division for effective training. Extensive experiments on various datasets in Habitat simulator show our AwareVLN significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/.

</details>
